------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE carrega_combo
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE carrega_combo
LParameters _Grupo, _Objeto, _Alias, _Branco, _Todos

If _Todos
	If fAcessoContab(Usuar,'C',_Grupo)
		=Seek(_Grupo,'CrSigCdGcr','Codigos')
		If CrSigCdGcr.BlqCongvs = 1
			Return .t.
		Endif	
		With _Objeto
			_Inclui = .t.
			For _Cont = 1 to .ListCount
				If .List( _Cont ) = _Grupo 
					_Inclui = .f.
					Exit
				Endif
			Next
			If _Todos 
				If .ListCount = 1 and _Todos
					.additem(Space(10) + ' - ' + 'TODOS OS GRUPOS' )
				EndIf 
			EndIf 
			If _Inclui
				.additem( _Grupo + ' - ' + CRSigCdGcr.Descrs )
				If _Grupo = CRSigCdPam.GrPadClis
					.ListIndex = _Cont
				Endif
			EndIf
			.Value = Space(10) + ' - ' + 'TODOS OS GRUPOS'
		Endwith
	EndIf
Else
	If _Branco
		_Objeto.AddItem(Space(10) + ' - Todos')
	EndIf

	If fAcessoContab(Usuar,'C',_Grupo)
		=Seek(_Grupo,'CrSigCdGcr','Codigos')
		If CrSigCdGcr.BlqCongvs = 1
			Return .t.
		Endif	
		With _Objeto
			_Inclui = .t.
			For _Cont = 1 to .ListCount
				If .List( _Cont ) = _Grupo 
					_Inclui = .f.
					Exit
				Endif
			Next
			If _Inclui 
				.additem( _Grupo + ' - ' + CRSigCdGcr.Descrs )
				If _Grupo = CRSigCdPam.GrPadClis
					.ListIndex = _Cont
				Endif
			Endif
			If _Cont = 1 or _Grupo = CRSigCdPam.GrPadClis
				.Value = _Grupo + ' - ' + CRSigCdGcr.Descrs
				If Type( '_Alias' ) = 'C'
					Select &_Alias
					Set Key to Left( .Value, 10 )
				Endif
			Endif
		Endwith
	EndIf
EndIf 
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE checaibge
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE checaibge
Lparameters pMun, pUFs, pObC, pObU

lnRtC = 9999999

lcQuery = [Select a.Codigos ] + ;
		    [From SigCdMun a, SigCdUfs b ] + ;
		   [Where a.Descs = '] + Padr(fLimpaTexto(Upper(pMun)), 40) + [' And ] + ;
		   		 [a.UFIBGEs = b.UFIBGEs And ] + ;
		   		 [b.Estados = '] + pUFs + [']

If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, [LocalMunic]) < 1)
	=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (LocalMunic)])
	Return 0
EndIf

Select LocalMunic
Go Top In LocalMunic
If Not Eof([LocalMunic])
	lnRtC = Int(Val(LocalMunic.Codigos))
EndIf

pObC.Value = lnRtC
pObC.Refresh
pObC.Forecolor = Rgb(0,0,0)
If (lnRtC = 9999999)
	pObC.Forecolor = Rgb(255,0,0)
EndIf

lnRtU = 99

lcQuery = [Select UFIBGEs ] + ;
		    [From SigCdUfs ] + ;
		   [Where Estados = '] + Padr(pUFs, 2) + [']

If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, [LocalUF]) < 1)
	=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (LocalUF)])
	Return 0
EndIf

Select LocalUF
Go Top In LocalUF
If Not Eof([LocalUF])
	lnRtU = LocalUF.UFIBGEs
EndIf

pObU.Value = lnRtU
pObU.Refresh
pObU.Forecolor = Rgb(0,0,0)
If (lnRtU = 99)
	pObU.Forecolor = Rgb(255,0,0)
EndIf
* Fim ********************************************************************************************
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE checasenha
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE checasenha
ThisForm.UsuVendedor = []

lcQuery = [Select Dopes ] + ;
		    [From SigOpCdd ] + ;
		   [Where LibcSens = 3]

If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, [crOpe]) < 1)
	=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (crOpe)])
	Return .f.
EndIf
Select crOpe
Go Top In crOpe
If Not Eof([crOpe])
	Do Form SigOpCks With [MOVESTOQUE], [Libera Consulta de Vendas], [] To _Retorno
	llOk = (Left(_Retorno, 1) = "*")
	If Not llOk
		=MessageBox([Autorização Não Confirmada!!!], 0+48, [Senha Inválida])
		Return .f.
	EndIf
	ThisForm.UsuVendedor = Substr(_Retorno, 2)
EndIf

If Not Empty(ThisForm.UsuVendedor)
	If Not fChecaAcesso([SIGCDCTA], [VENDEDOR], .f., ThisForm.UsuVendedor)
		lcQuery = [Select ContaVens ] + ;
				    [From SigCdCli ] + ;
				   [Where IClis = '] + Padr(ThisForm.fld_Principal.pag_Consulta.Origem.get_Conta.Value, 10) + [']

		If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, [crCli]) < 1)
			=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (crCli)])
			Return .f.
		EndIf
		Select crCli
		Go Top In crCli
		If Not Eof([crCli]) And (Not Empty(crCli.ContaVens) And (crCli.ContaVens <> ThisForm.UsuVendedor))
			=MessageBox([Você Não Possui Acesso a Essa Consulta!!!], 0+48, [Consulta Inválida])
			Return .f.
		EndIf
	EndIf
EndIf

Return .t.
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE filtratipos
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE filtratipos
Local lcTipos, lcArea, lcFiltro

lcTipos = []

Go Top in crTmpTipos
Do While not Eof([crTmpTipos])
	If crTmpTipos.Listas = 1
		lcTipos = lcTipos + Iif(Empty(lcTipos),[( fPags = ],[ or fPags = ]) + Chr(39) + crTmpTipos.fPags + Chr(39)
	Endif
	Skip +1 in crTmpTipos
EndDo
If not Empty(lcTipos)
	lcTipos = lcTipos + [)]
	lcFiltro = [Set Filter to ] + lcTipos + [ in TmpPar]
	&lcFiltro
	Select TmpPar
	Locate	
	ThisForm.Fld_principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Pendencias.grd_Pendente.Refresh()
Else
	Select TmpPar
	Set Filter To 0=1
	Locate	
	ThisForm.Fld_principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Pendencias.grd_Pendente.Refresh()
Endif

lnSum = 0
Select TmpPar
Scan
	lnSum = lnSum + TmpPar.Valos
EndScan
Go Top In TmpPar
ThisForm.Fld_principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Pendencias.getTotPends.Value = lnSum

Return



ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE filtrooppend
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE filtrooppend
Parameters _Tipo
Public lcFitlerT

Do Case
	Case _tipo = 1
		Select dbOperaP
		If Empty(Left(ThisForm.fld_principal.pag_resultado.result.conserto.cmb_Grupo.Value,10))
			Set Filter To 
			Go top
		Else
			Set Filter To dbOperaP.Grupos = Left(ThisForm.fld_principal.pag_resultado.result.conserto.cmb_Grupo.Value,10)
		EndIf  
		Go Top 

	Case _tipo = 2
	
		ThisForm.fld_principal.pag_resultado.result.conserto.grd_conserto.RecordSource = ''
		If Used('dbConsertoP')
			Use in dbConsertoP
		EndIf 
		
		
		ThisForm.poDataMgr.CursorQuery([SigOpPed], [CrSigOpPed], [Dopes], dbOperaP.Dopes, [Tpagos,Tutils])

		If Not Used([CrSigOpPed])
			=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (CursorQuery - CrSigOpPed)])
			Return 0
		EndIf

		lcQuery = 'Select *  from dbConserto Where' + ;
					 Iif(Alltrim(dbOperaP.Grupos)<>'|',' dopes = dbOperaP.Dopes And ','') + ;
					 Iif(CrSigOpPed.Tpagos = 1,' ChkPagos = .t. And ',Iif(CrSigOpPed.Tpagos = 2,' ChkPagos = .F. And ','')) +;
					 Iif(CrSigOpPed.Tutils = 1,' Utilizados = 1 ',Iif(CrSigOpPed.Tutils = 2,' Utilizados = 0 ','')) 

		
		If Right(Alltrim(lcQuery),3) = 'And'
			lcQuery = Substr(lcQuery,1,Len(Alltrim(lcQuery ))-3)
		EndIf
		
		If Right(Alltrim(lcQuery),5) = 'Where'
			lcQuery = Substr(lcQuery,1,Len(Alltrim(lcQuery ))-5)
		EndIf 

					 
		lcQuery = lcQuery + ' Into Cursor dbConsertoP Order by Datas '

		&lcQuery
		
		With ThisForm.fld_principal.pag_resultado.result.conserto.grd_conserto
			.RecordSource = 'dbConsertoP'
			.Column1.ControlSource = 'mascnum'
			.Column2.ControlSource = 'datas'
			.Column3.ControlSource = 'emps'
			.Column4.ControlSource = 'Status'
			.Column5.ControlSource = 'ObsCon'
			.Column6.ControlSource = 'ValPends'	
		EndWith

EndCase
	

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE impressao
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE impressao
LParameters _Saida, _LSaldo, _LLimite, _LChequePend, _LChequeDev, _LChequeDep, ;
			_LCartao, _LPend, _LOper, _Termica, _LChqNeg, _LResVenda, _LProdutos, _LProducao, _lEstoque

Create Cursor TmpRelGV ( ValTipo n(3), CabTipo c(70), Grupo c(10), Valor n(12,2), Moeda c(3),;
						Data d, Descricao c(50), Observa c(76), Opers c(1), Valopg n(12,2), Saldo n(12,2),;
						Cpros c(14), DPros c(40), Numes n(6), Dopes c(20), PrazoEnts d, Fase c(10), Qtds n(9,2), Nenvs n(10), ;
						Nops n(10), xper n(6,2), Emps c(3), Pesos n(9,2), Cbars n(14), Reffs c(40), Colecoes c(10),;
						Anos c(4), Meses c(2) )
Index on Str( ValTipo, 3 ) + Anos + Meses + Grupo Tag ValGrupo

Create Cursor TmpTotal ( Moeda c(3), Valor n(12,2) ) 
Index on Moeda Tag Moeda

Local _Tipo, _CodEmp

_CodEmp = ThisForm.fld_principal.pag_consulta.Origem.Get_Conta.Value

=ThisForm.Podatamgr.cursorquery('SigCdCli','CrTmpCli','Iclis',_CodEmp)

If _LSaldo
	Select dbSaldo
	_ValTipo = 1
	If _Termica
		_CabTipo = 'SALDOS DE CONTA CORRENTE'
	Else
		_CabTipo = 'Saldos de Conta Corrente'
	Endif
	Go Top
	If !Eof()
		Scan
			Insert Into TmpRelGv ( Cabtipo, Valtipo, Grupo, Valor, Moeda ) ;
				Values ( _CabTipo, _ValTipo, dbSaldo.Grupos, dbSaldo.Saldos, dbSaldo.Moedas )
		Endscan
	Else
		Insert Into TmpRelGv ( Cabtipo, Valtipo, Observa ) ;
			Values ( _CabTipo, _ValTipo, 'Conta não tem Saldo...' )
	Endif
Endif

If _LLimite
	If _Termica
		_CabTipo = 'LIMITE DE CREDITO'
	Else
		_CabTipo = 'Limite de Crédito'
	Endif
	_ValTipo = 2
	=ThisForm.PodataMgr.CursorQuery('SigClLcr','CrTmpLCrDi','Contas',_CodEmp)
	Select CrTmpLCrdi
	If Reccount() = 0
		Insert Into TmpRelGv ( Cabtipo, Valtipo, Observa ) ;
			Values ( _CabTipo, _ValTipo, 'Conta não tem Limite de Crédito...' )
	Else
		Scan 
			Insert Into TmpRelGv ( Cabtipo, Valtipo, Grupo, Valor, Moeda ) ;
				Values ( _CabTipo, _ValTipo, crtmpLcrdi.Grupos, crtmpLcrdi.LimCres, crtmpLcrdi.Moedas )
		Endscan
	Endif
Endif

If _LChequePend
	Select dbAberto
	Go Top
	_ValTipo = 3
	If _Termica
		_CabTipo = 'CHEQUES PENDENTES'
	Else
		_CabTipo = 'Cheques Pendentes'
	Endif
	If !Eof()
		Scan
			Insert Into TmpRelGv ( Cabtipo, Valtipo, Data, Valor, Descricao ) ;
				Values ( _CabTipo, _ValTipo, dbAberto.Vencs, dbAberto.Valors,; 
						dbAberto.Bancos + '.' + dbAberto.Agencias + '.' + ;
						dbAberto.nContas + '.' + dbAberto.nCheques )
			=ThisForm.PodataMgr.CursorQuery('SigCheh','CrSigCheh','Nums',DbAberto.Nums)
			Select CrSigCheH
			Index On Dtos(Datas) Tag Datas
			Go bott
			Replace TmpRelGv.Observa With Nvl(CRSigCheh.Hists, '') In TmpRelGv
		Endscan
	Else
		Insert Into TmpRelGv ( Cabtipo, Valtipo, Observa ) ;
			Values ( _CabTipo, _ValTipo, 'Conta não tem Cheques Pendentes...' )
	Endif
Endif

If _LChequeDev
	Select dbDevolvido
	Go Top
	_ValTipo = 4
	If _Termica
		_CabTipo = 'CHEQUES DEVOLVIDOS'
	Else
		_CabTipo = 'Cheques Devolvidos'
	Endif
	If !Eof()
		Scan
			Insert Into TmpRelGv ( Cabtipo, Valtipo, Data, Valor, Descricao ) ;
				Values ( _CabTipo, _ValTipo, dbDevolvido.Vencs, dbDevolvido.Valors,; 
						dbDevolvido.Bancos + '.' + dbDevolvido.Agencias + '.' + ;
						dbDevolvido.nContas + '.' + dbDevolvido.nCheques )
		Endscan
	Else
		Insert Into TmpRelGv ( Cabtipo, Valtipo, Observa ) ;
			Values ( _CabTipo, _ValTipo, 'Conta não tem Cheques Devolvidos...' )
	Endif
Endif

If _LChequeDep
	=ThisForm.PodataMgr.CursorQuery('SigChe','CrTmpChe','Iclis',_CodEmp)
	Select CrTmpChe
	Go Top
	_ValTipo = 5
	If _Termica
		_CabTipo = 'CHEQUES DEPOSITADOS'
	Else
		_CabTipo = 'Cheques Depositados'
	Endif
	If !Eof()
		Scan While Iclis = _CodEmp for Umovs = 'DEPÓSITO'
			Insert Into TmpRelGv ( Cabtipo, Valtipo, Data, Valor, Descricao ) ;
				Values ( _CabTipo, _ValTipo, crTmpChe.Vencs, CrTmpChe.Valors,; 
						CrTmpChe.Bancos + '.' + CrTmpChe.Agencias + '.' + ;
						CrTmpChe.nContas + '.' + CrTmpChe.nCheques )
		Endscan
	Else
		Insert Into TmpRelGv ( Cabtipo, Valtipo, Observa ) ;
			Values ( _CabTipo, _ValTipo, 'Conta não tem Cheques Depositados...' )
	Endif
Endif

If _LCartao
	Select TmpCart
	Go Top
	_ValTipo = 9
	If _Termica
		_CabTipo = 'CARTOES PENDENTES'
	Else
		_CabTipo = 'Cartões Pendentes'
	Endif
	If !Eof()
		Scan
			Insert Into TmpRelGv ( Cabtipo, Valtipo, Data, Valor, Descricao ) ;
				Values ( _CabTipo, _ValTipo, TmpCart.Vencs, TmpCart.Valos,; 
						Alltrim( TmpCart.Fpags ) + ' ( ' + Alltrim( TmpCart.Cartaos ) + ' ) - ' + ;
						Alltrim( TmpCart.NoCreditos ) )
		Endscan
	Else
		Insert Into TmpRelGv ( Cabtipo, Valtipo, Observa ) ;
			Values ( _CabTipo, _ValTipo, 'Conta não tem Cartoes Pendentes...' )
	Endif
Endif

If _LPend
	Select TmpPar
	Go Top
	_ValTipo = 7
	If _Termica
		_CabTipo = 'PENDENCIAS'
	Else
		_CabTipo = 'Pendências'
	Endif
	If !Eof()
		Scan
			Insert Into TmpRelGv ( Cabtipo, Valtipo, Data, Valor, Descricao, Moeda, Opers ) ;
				Values ( _CabTipo, _ValTipo, TmpPar.Vencs, TmpPar.Valos,; 
						TmpPar.Hists, TmpPar.Moefpgs, TmpPar.Opers )
			If !Seek( TmpPar.Moefpgs, 'TmpTotal' )
				Insert Into TmpTotal ( Moeda ) Values ( TmpPar.Moefpgs )
			Endif
			Replace TmpTotal.Valor With TmpTotal.Valor + ( TmpPar.Valos * Iif( TmpPar.Opers = 'C', -1, 1 ) )
		Endscan
		If !_Termica Or _Saida = 'V' 
			Select TmpTotal
			Scan
				Insert Into TmpRelGv ( Valtipo, Valor, Moeda ) ;
							Value ( _ValTipo, TmpTotal.Valor, TmpTotal.Moeda )
			Endscan
		Endif
	Else
		Insert Into TmpRelGv ( Cabtipo, Valtipo, Observa ) ;
			Values ( _CabTipo, _ValTipo, 'Conta não tem Pendencias...' )
	Endif
Endif

If _LOper
	Select dbConserto
	Go Top
	_ValTipo = 8
	If _Termica
		_CabTipo = 'OPERACOES PENDENTES'
	Else
		_CabTipo = 'Operações Pendentes'
	Endif
	If !Eof()
		Scan
			Insert Into TmpRelGv ( Cabtipo, Valtipo, Data, Descricao ) ;
				Values ( _CabTipo, _ValTipo, dbConserto.Datas,; 
						Alltrim( dbConserto.Emps ) + Alltrim( dbConserto.Dopes ) + ' - ' + ;
						Alltrim( dbConserto.MascNum ) + ' ( ' + ;
						Alltrim( dbConserto.Status ) + ')' )
		Endscan
	Else
		Insert Into TmpRelGv ( Cabtipo, Valtipo, Observa ) ;
			Values ( _CabTipo, _ValTipo, 'Conta não tem Operacoes Pendentes...' )
	Endif
Endif

If _LChqNeg
	Select dbTitulo
	Go Top
	_ValTipo = 6
	If _Termica
		_CabTipo = 'CHEQUES NEGOCIADOS'
	Else
		_CabTipo = 'Cheques Negociados'
	Endif
	If !Eof()
		Scan
			Insert Into TmpRelGv ( Cabtipo, Valtipo, Grupo, Data, Valor, ValoPg, Saldo, Moeda, Observa ) ;
				Values ( _CabTipo, _ValTipo, dbtitulo.titulos, dbtitulo.vencs, dbtitulo.Valors,; 
						dbtitulo.ValPags, dbtitulo.Saldos, dbtitulo.moedas, Nvl(dbtitulo.hists, '') )
		Endscan
	Else
		Insert Into TmpRelGv ( Cabtipo, Valtipo, Observa ) ;
			Values ( _CabTipo, _ValTipo, 'Conta não tem Cheques Negociados...' )
	Endif
EndIf

If _LResVenda
	Select dbTotVendas
	Go Top
	_ValTipo = 10
	If _Termica
		_CabTipo = 'RESUMO DE VENDAS'
	Else
		_CabTipo = 'Resumo de Vendas'
	Endif
	If !Eof()
		Scan
			Insert Into TmpRelGv ( CabTipo, ValTipo, Grupo, Moeda, Valor, ValoPg, Anos, Meses ) ;
				Values ( _CabTipo, _ValTipo, DbTotVendas.mesanos, dbtotvendas.moedas, ;
					dbtotvendas.totals, dbtotvendas.vrmovs, DbTotVendas.Anos, DbTotVendas.Meses)
		Endscan
	Else
		Insert Into TmpRelGv ( Cabtipo, Valtipo, Observa ) ;
			Values ( _CabTipo, _ValTipo, 'Conta não tem Resumo de Vendas...' )
	Endif
EndIf

If _Lprodutos
	Select dbProduto
	Go Top
	_ValTipo = 11
	llDtini = ThisForm.fld_principal.pag_resultado.SelecionaImpressão.Cnt_ProdAdq.Dt_inicial.Value
	llDtFin = ThisForm.fld_principal.pag_resultado.SelecionaImpressão.Cnt_ProdAdq.Dt_final.Value
	_CabTipo = 'Produtos Adquiridos Pelo Cliente: '+Dtoc(llDtIni)+' - '+Dtoc(llDtFin)
	If !Eof()
		Scan
			If Not Between(DbProduto.Datas,llDtini,llDtFin)
				Loop
			EndIf
			=ThisForm.podatamgr.Cursorquery('SigCdPro','CrTmpPro','Cpros',DbProduto.Cpros,[Cpros, Reffs, Colecoes])
			Insert Into TmpRelGv ( CabTipo, ValTipo, Cpros, DPros, Qtds, Opers, Emps, dopes, Numes, Data, Valor, Valopg, ;
								   Saldo, Pesos, Cbars, Reffs, Colecoes ) ;
				Values ( _CabTipo, _ValTipo, DbProduto.Cpros, dbProduto.DPros, DbProduto.Qtds, DbProduto.Opers, DbProduto.Emps, ;
						DbProduto.Dopes, Val(DbProduto.mascnum), DbProduto.Datas, DbProduto.Totas, DbProduto.Origtotas,;
						DbProduto.BasTotas, DbProduto.Pesos, DbProduto.Codbarras, CrTmpPro.Reffs, CrTmpPro.Colecoes)
		Endscan
	Else
		Insert Into TmpRelGv ( Cabtipo, Valtipo, Observa ) ;
			Values ( _CabTipo, _ValTipo, 'Conta não Tem Produtos Adquiridos...' )
	Endif
EndIf

If _Lproducao
	Select dbproduc
	Set Order To Nenvs
	Go Top
	_ValTipo = 12
	If _Termica
		_CabTipo = 'Posicao de Produtos Em Producao'
	Else
		_CabTipo = 'Posição de Produtos Em Produção'
	Endif
	If !Eof()
		Scan
			=Seek(DbProduc.Emps + DbProduc.Dopes + Str(DbProduc.Numes,6),'DbConserto','EmpDopNum')
			Insert Into TmpRelGv ( CabTipo, ValTipo, Dopes, Numes, Data, PrazoEnts, Cpros, Fase, Qtds,;
								   Nenvs, Nops, xPer) ;
				Values ( _CabTipo, _ValTipo, DbProduc.dopes, DbProduc.Numes, dbConserto.Datas, DbConserto.Prazoents,;
						DbProduc.Cpros, DbProduc.Fase, DbProduc.Qtde, DbProduc.Nenvs, DbProduc.Nops, DbProduc.xper)
		Endscan
	Else
		Insert Into TmpRelGv ( Cabtipo, Valtipo, Observa ) ;
			Values ( _CabTipo, _ValTipo, 'Conta não Tem Produtos em Produção...' )
	Endif
EndIf

If _lEstoque
	Select dbEstoque
	Set Order To
	Set Order to Grupos
	Go Top
	_ValTipo = 13
	If _Termica
		_CabTipo = 'Posicao de Estoque '
	Else
		_CabTipo = 'Posição de Estoque '
	Endif
	If !Eof()
		Scan
			Insert Into TmpRelGv ( CabTipo, ValTipo, Grupo, Cpros, Qtds ) ;
				Values ( _CabTipo, _ValTipo, dbEstoque.Grupos, dbEstoque.Cpros, DbEstoque.sQtds)
		Endscan
	Else
		Insert Into TmpRelGv ( Cabtipo, Valtipo, Observa ) ;
			Values ( _CabTipo, _ValTipo, 'Conta não Tem Produtos em Estoque...' )
	Endif
EndIf


Select TmpRelGv
Go Top

If Eof()
	Messagebox( 'Não há dados para imprimir...', 48, '' )
	Return
Endif

If _Termica
	Insert Into TmpRelGv ( ValTipo ) Value ( 999 )
Endif

If _Saida = 'V'
	Report Form SIGOPCGV Preview  NoConsole
Else
	If _Termica
		ThisForm.ImpTermica
	Else
		Report Form SIGOPCGV to Print Prompt Noconsole
	Endif
Endif

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE impressaocad
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE impressaocad
LParameters _Saida, _LGenerico,_LPessoais,_LBancarias,_LFollowup,_Termica
Private _Tipo, _CodEmp, _varRB, _varH 

pGenerico = _LGenerico
pPessoais = _LPessoais
pbancaria = _LBancarias
pFollowup = _LFollowup

_varRB = ""
_varH = ""

_CodEmp = ThisForm.fld_Principal.pag_consulta.origem.get_conta.value 

If _LBancarias
	lcQuery = [Select * from SigCdCeb where iClis = ']+_CodEmp+[']
	ThisForm.poDataMgr.SQLExecute(lcQuery,[crSigCdCeb])
	Select crSigCdCeb
	if eof()
		_varRB = "Não Constam Referências Bancarias Referentes ao Cliente!"
	else	
		Do while _CodEmp = IClis .and. !eof()
			_varRB = _varRB + crSigCdCeb.nBancos + " " + subs(crSigCdCeb.Bancos,1,22) + " " + ;
							crSigCdCeb.nAgencias + " " + subs(crSigCdCeb.Agencias,1,22) + " " +;
							crSigCdCeb.bTels + " " + crSigCdCeb.Ramals
			skip
		enddo
	endif	
Endif

If _LFollowup
	lcQuery = [Select * from SigCdClh where iClis = ']+_CodEmp+[']
	ThisForm.poDataMgr.SQLExecute(lcQuery,[crSigCdClh])
	Select crSigCdClh

	if eof()
		_varH = "Não Constam Follow-up Referentes ao Cliente!"
	else
		Do while _CodEmp = IClis .and. !eof()
			_varH = _varH + Ttoc(crSigCdClh.Datas) + " " + crSigCdClh.Hists
			skip
		enddo
	endif	
Endif

Select * From crSigCdCli Into Cursor crCli
Select crCli
Go Top In crCli
If _Saida = 'V'
	Report Form SigReGcd Preview NoConsole 
Else
	If _Termica
		ThisForm.ImpTermicad
	Else
		Report Form SigReGcd to Print Prompt Noconsole
	Endif
Endif


ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE imptermica
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE imptermica
Do Form SigOpIbo With ThisForm,7
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE imptermicad
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE imptermicad
Local llObsDevs, lcObs, lcObs1, lnObs1, CRLF

CRLF = Chr(13) + Chr(10)

llFechaCimp = .f.
llFechaAcImp = .f.
llFechaSigCdAcG = .f.

If Aprinters( gaprinters ) > 0
	ok=.f.

	llFlags = .t.

	If ! Used('crSigCdmp')
		lcQuery = [Select * from SigCdmp]
		ThisForm.poDataMgr.SQLExecute(lcQuery,[crSigCdmp])		
		llFechaCimp = .t.
	EndIf
	If ! Used('SigSyImp')
		lcQuery = [Select * from SigSyImp]
		ThisForm.poDataMgr.SQLExecute(lcQuery,[crSigSyImp])
		llFechaAcImp = .t.
	EndIf
	If ! Used('SigCdAcG')
		lcQuery = [Select * from SigCdAcG]
		ThisForm.poDataMgr.SQLExecute(lcQuery,[crSigCdAcG])
		llFechaSigCdAcG = .t.
	Endif

	Select * From crSigCdmp Where nTpImpres = 1 Into Cursor LocalCimp

	Select b.* From crSigSyImp a, crSigCdmp b Where a.UsuAcess = Usuar And a.cimps = b.impres ;
			Union all ;
			Select c.* From crSigCdAcG a, crSigSyImp b, crSigCdmp c Where a.Usuarios = Usuar ;
			And a.Grupos = b.GrAcess And b.Cimps = c.Impres Into Cursor CrTmpCImp

	Select CrTmpCimp

	If Reccount() > 0
		if Used('LocalcImp')
			use in LocalcImp
		Endif
		Select * From CrTmpCimp Into Cursor LocalcImp1
	    USE Dbf('LocalcImp1') Alias LocalcImp Again In 0
		Select LocalCImp
		Index On Impres Tag skImpres
	Endif

	Select LocalcImp
	
	Select *, llFlags as lMarcas, llFlags as lSituas ;
	  From LocalcImp ;
	  Into Cursor csSigCdmp1

	if Used('csSigCdmp')
		use in csSigCdmp
	Endif

    USE Dbf('csSigCdmp1') Alias csSigCdmp Again In 0

	Select csSigCdmp
	Index On Impres Tag Imprime For ( lSituas = .T.)
	Index On Impres Tag Impres
	Goto Top In csSigCdmp

	If (Reccount() # 1 And Reccount() # 0)	&& Se Tiver Mais Que Uma Impressora, Habilita Formulário
		Do Form SigOpIpb	&& Seleciona Impressora Para Etiqueta.
		Ok=.t.
	EndIf

	If Ok
		Ok=(Ascan(gaprinters,Alltrim(csSigCdmp.Impres))<>0)
	Endif
	
	If !ok
		Messagebox( 'Não existe impressora para boleto!', 48, '' )
		Return
	Endif

Else
	Messagebox( 'Não existe impressora para boleto!', 48, '' )
	Return
Endif

pFontes=Iif(IsEmpty(csSigCdmp.fontes),'chr(13)',csSigCdmp.fontes)
pCond=Iif(IsEmpty(csSigCdmp.fontes),'chr(13)',csSigCdmp.fontes)
pExpA=Iif(IsEmpty(csSigCdmp.expas),'chr(13)',csSigCdmp.expas)
pExpD=Iif(IsEmpty(csSigCdmp.expds),'chr(13)',csSigCdmp.expds)
pMaxCols=Iif(IsEmpty(csSigCdmp.maxcols),'chr(13)',csSigCdmp.maxcols)
llObsDevs = .F.
lnTamEnd = pMaxCols-(Len(Alltrim(crSigCdCli.Nums))+2)

Set Printer to Name ( Alltrim( csSigCdmp.Impres ) )
??? &pFontes + CRLF

***************    Cabeçalho do Relatório    *******************
??? &pExpA + Repl( '-', Int( pMaxCols / 2 ) ) + CRLF + ;
				Padc( 'Consulta', Int( pMaxCols / 2 ) ) + CRLF + ;
				Padc( 'Ficha Cadastral', Int( pMaxCols / 2 ) ) + CRLF + ;
				Repl( '-', Int( pMaxCols / 2 ) ) + &pExpD + CRLF + CRLF

**************    Código e Nome do Cliente    ******************
??? Padr( 'Conta : ' + crSigCdCli.Iclis + ' - ' + Alltrim( crSigCdCli.Rclis ), pMaxCols ) + CRLF + CRLF

****************      Dados do Cliente      ********************
if pGenerico
	??? &pExpA + Padc( 'Dados do Cliente', Int( pMaxCols / 2 ) ) + &pExpD + CRLF

	??? 'Razao Social:'	+ CRLF
	??? subs(crSigCdCli.Razaos,1,pMaxCols) + CRLF + CRLF

	??? 'Data    : ' + dtoc(crSigCdCli.DataIncs) + CRLF
	??? 'CGC/CPF : ' + crSigCdCli.CPFs + CRLF
	??? 'R.G.    : ' + crSigCdCli.RGs  + CRLF

	??? 'Endereco: ' + CRLF
	??? Alltrim(subs(crSigCdCli.Endes,1,lnTamEnd)) + ', ' + Alltrim(crSigCdCli.Nums) + CRLF
	??? alltrim(crSigCdCli.Bairs) + ' - ' + subs(crSigCdCli.Cidas,1,19) + CRLF
	??? 'CEP     : ' + crSigCdCli.CEPs + ' / ' + crSigCdCli.Estas + CRLF

	??? 'Telefone: ' + alltrim(crSigCdCli.Tel1s)
	if !empt(crSigCdCli.Tel2s)
		??? ' / ' + crSigCdCli.Tel2s + CRLF
	else
		??? CRLF	
	endif
	??? 'Fax     : ' + crSigCdCli.Faxs + CRLF
	??? 'e-mail  : ' + Alltrim(crSigCdCli.emails) + CRLF
	??? replicate('-',pMaxCols-1) + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
endif

****************    Dados Pessoais      ********************
if pPessoais 
	??? &pExpA + Padc( 'Dados Pessoais', Int( pMaxCols / 2 ) ) + &pExpD + CRLF

	??? 'Comercial   :'	+ CRLF
	??? subs(crSigCdCli.EndTrabs,1,pMaxCols) + CRLF
	if !empt(crSigCdCli.Pais) .or. !empt(crSigCdCli.Maes)
		??? 'Pai / Mae   :'	+ CRLF
	endif	
	if !empt(crSigCdCli.Pais)
		??? subs(crSigCdCli.Pais,1,pMaxCols) + CRLF
	endif	
	if !empt(crSigCdCli.Maes)
		??? subs(crSigCdCli.Maes,1,pMaxCols) + CRLF
	endif	
	??? 'Nacional.   :' + crSigCdCli.Nacionals + CRLF
	??? 'Nascimento  :' + dtoc(crSigCdCli.Nascs) + CRLF
	??? 'Profissao   :' + crSigCdCli.Profiss + CRLF
	??? 'Estado Civil:' + crSigCdCli.EstCivils + CRLF
	if !empt(crSigCdCli.Conjuges)
		??? 'Conjuge     :' + crSigCdCli.Conjuges + CRLF
		??? 'Casamento   :' + dtoc(crSigCdCli.DtCasas) + ;
			' Nasc.: ' + dtoc(crSigCdCli.DtNCons) + CRLF
		??? 'R.G.        :' + crSigCdCli.RGConjuges + CRLF
	endif	
	??? replicate('-',pMaxCols-1)
	??? Chr(13) + Chr(10)
endif

**************   Referencias Bancarias    ******************
if pBancaria
	??? &pExpA + Padc( 'Referencias Bancarias', Int( pMaxCols / 2 ) ) + &pExpD + CRLF

	lcQuery = [Select * from SigCdCeb where iClis = ']+_CodEmp+[']
	ThisForm.poDataMgr.SQLExecute(lcQuery,[crSigCdCeb])
	Select crSigCdCeb

	if eof()
		??? '* NAO Constam Referencias Bancarias! *' + CRLF
	else	
		??? '  Banco  Age.      Telefone      Ram.   ' + CRLF
		??? '  ----- ------ ---------------- ------  ' + CRLF
		*       999   9999   99999999999999   9999
		Do while _CodEmp = IClis .and. !eof()
			??? "   " + crSigCdCeb.nBancos + "   " + crSigCdCeb.nAgencias +;
				"   " + crSigCdCeb.bTels + "   " + crSigCdCeb.Ramals + CRLF
			skip
		enddo
	endif	
	??? replicate('-',pMaxCols-1)
	??? Chr(13) + Chr(10)
endif

*******************      Follow-Up     *********************
if pFollowup
	??? &pExpA + Padc( 'Follow-up', Int( pMaxCols / 2 ) ) + &pExpD + CRLF

	lcQuery = [Select * from SigCdClh where iClis = ']+_CodEmp+[']
	ThisForm.poDataMgr.SQLExecute(lcQuery,[crSigCdClh])
	Select crSigCdClh

	if eof()
		??? '* NAO Constam Follow-up! *' + CRLF
	else
		??? '   Data      Hora        Historico      ' + CRLF
		??? '---------- -------- --------------------' + CRLF
		*    99/99/9999 99:99:99 99999999999999999999
		*	             9999999999999999999999999999
		*	 			 9999999999999999999999999999
		Do while _CodEmp = IClis .and. !eof()
			??? Ttoc(crSigCdClh.Datas) + " " 
			??? subs(crSigCdClh.Hists,1,20) + CRLF
			if alltrim(subs(crSigCdClh.Hists,21,28)) # ""
				??? space(12) + subs(crSigCdClh.Hists,21,28) + CRLF
			endif
			if alltrim(subs(crSigCdClh.Hists,21,28)) # ""
				??? space(12) + subs(crSigCdClh.Hists,49,28) + CRLF
			endif
			skip
		enddo
	endif	
	??? replicate('-',pMaxCols-1)
endif


For _xLinha = 1 to 5
	??? CRLF
Next

If llFechaCimp
	USE In crSigCdmp
EndIf
If llFechaAcImp
	USE In crSigSyImp
Endif
If llFechaSigCdAcG
	USE In crSigCdAcG 
Endif


Set Printer to Default
Return

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE mativacredito
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE mativacredito
local lcCdCliente, lcDsCliente, lcNrCpf
local llSemDados, _totabe, _totdev, lcArquivo1
llthispage=Thisform.fld_principal.pag_resultado.result.credito.pgCredito.consultas
If Not thisform.pccredito
	thisform.pccredito=.t. 
	llthispage.Grade.RecordSource = " "

	With thisform.fld_principal.pag_consulta.origem
		lcCdCliente = .Get_conta.value
		lcDsCliente = .Get_dconta.value
		lcNrCpf     = .Get_cpf.value
	EndWith
	lcnrCpf = Strtran(Strtran(Strtran(lcnrCpf,".",""),"-",""),"/","")
	lcNrCpf = Iif(Len(Alltrim(lcNrCpf))=11,Transform(lcNrCpf,'@R 999.999.999-99'),;
	          Iif(Len(Alltrim(lcNrCpf))=14,Transform(lcNrCpf,'@R 99.999.999/9999-99'),lcNrCpf))

	lcQuery = 'Select * ' + ;
				'From SigCdCli ' + ;
			   'Where Cpfs = "'+lcNrCpf+'"'

	ThisForm.poDataMgr.SQLExecute(lcQuery,'crCli')

	If used('crSigFiCp')
		use in crSigFiCp
	EndIf

	lcQuery  = 'Select * From SigFiCp Where ccpfs = "'+lcNrCpf+'"' 

	ThisForm.poDataMgr.SQLExecute(lcQuery,'crSigFiCp')

	Select crSigFiCp
	Index On idchep Tag xidchep

	Select crSigFiCp
	Go top 

	With llthispage
		With .Grade
			.RecordSource = "crSigFiCp"
			.ColumnCount = 6
			.ReadOnly = .t.
			.Column1.ControlSource = "crSigFiCp.Tipos"
			.Column2.ControlSource = "crSigFiCp.Usuars"
			.Column3.ControlSource = "crSigFiCp.Emps"
			.Column4.ControlSource = "crSigFiCp.Datas"
			.Column5.ControlSource = "crSigFiCp.Dopes"
			.Column6.ControlSource = "crSigFiCp.Numes"
			.setfocus
		EndWith
		With .Cnt_Valor
			.get_nr_cpf.ControlSource = "crSigFiCp.ccpfs"
			.get_valor.ControlSource  = "crSigFiCp.nvalors"
			.dtvenc.ControlSource	  = "crSigFiCp.dvencs"
			.dtvenc.StrictDateEntry   = 0
			.dtvenc.Format            = "E"
		EndWith
		With .Cnt_Banco
			.get_bco.ControlSource     = "crSigFiCp.cbancos"
			.get_age.ControlSource     = "crSigFiCp.cagencias"
			.get_conta.ControlSource   = "crSigFiCp.ccontas"
			.get_num.ControlSource     = "crSigFiCp.ccheinis"
			.get_dig.ControlSource     = "crSigFiCp.cdiginis"
			.get_numf.ControlSource    = "crSigFiCp.cchefins"
			.get_digf.ControlSource    = "crSigFiCp.cdigfins"
			.get_serie.ControlSource   = "crSigFiCp.cseries"
			.get_dtctaab.ControlSource = "crSigFiCp.cdaberts"
		EndWith
		With .Cnt_Docs
			.OptDocs.ControlSource     = "crSigFiCp.nChkDocs"
			.Get_Data.ControlSource    = "crSigFiCp.tdDocs"
		EndWith 
	EndWith
	
	lcColor = 'RGB(0,0,255)'
	lcLabel = ''
	lcQuery = 'Select Descrs ' + ;
				'From SigCdCst ' + ;
			   'Where codigos = "'+padr(crCli.Situas,3)+'"'

	ThisForm.poDataMgr.SQLExecute(lcQuery,'crScli')
	if RecCount("crSCli") > 0
		lcLabel = crSCli.Descrs
		If crCli.Situas = '*'
			lcColor = 'RGB(255,0,0)'
		Endif
	else
		lcLabel = ''
	Endif

	With Thisform.fld_principal.pag_Resultado.result.credito
		.Label2.Caption = alltrim(lcLabel)
		.Label2.ForeColor = &lcColor
	EndWith
	
	ThisForm.poDataMgr.SQLExecute('select codigos,descrs,codpai,visuals from SigFiCt order by 1','crSigFiCT')
	Index On codigos Tag codigos
	Set Order To ('') In ('crSigFiCT')

EndIf 

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE mativahcheque
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE mativahcheque
llthispage=Thisform.fld_principal.pag_resultado.result.hcheques


llDatai = llthispage.Cnt_periodo.Dt_inicial.Value
llDataf = llthispage.cnt_periodo.Dt_final.Value
llDatai = fDtoSQL(llDatai)
llDataf = fDtoSQL(llDataf,'23:59:59')

llthispage.GradeCheque.RecordSource = ' '
llthispage.GradeMovimenta.RecordSource = ' '
llthispage.GradeProrrog.RecordSource = ' '


lcWherChe = [ select b.dtents,a.*,d.Nums as Tipo,VENCS=case when c.tipos = 'CR' then c.DTENTS ]+;
			[ when c.tipos = 'DB' then getdate() end from SigChe a join ]+;
			[ (select max(dtents) as dtents, nums  from SigCqChm group by nums ) b on  a.nums = b.nums ]+;
			[ join SigCqChm c on b.nums = c.nums and b.dtents = c.dtents ]+;
			[ left join SigCheh d On a.Nums = d.Nums ]

*If !Empty(thisform.fld_principal.pag_consulta.origem.get_conta.Value)
	lcCliente = thisform.fld_principal.pag_consulta.origem.get_conta.Value
	lcWherChe = lcWherChe + [ Where a.Iclis = ?lcCliente Order By a.Vencs ]
	*lcWherChe = [Select a.*, b.Nums as Tipo From SigChe a Left Join SigCheh b On a.Nums = b.Nums Where a.Iclis = ?lcCliente Order By a.Vencs]
*Else
	*lcWherChe = [Select a.*, b.Nums as Tipo From SigChe a Left Join SigCheh b On a.Nums = b.Nums Where a.Vencs Between ?llDatai And ?llDataf Order By a.Vencs]
*	lcWherChe = lcWherChe + [ Where a.Vencs Between ?llDatai And ?llDataf Order By a.Vencs ]	
*Endif

ThisForm.podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcWherChe,'CrSigCheT')

Select *, 000000 AS DiasP from CrSigCheT into cursor CrSigChe readwrite 
Select CrSigChe
Scan 
	Replace DiasP with Iif(Ttod(CrSigChe.dtents)-Ttod(CrSigChe.Vencs)<=0,0,Ttod(CrSigChe.dtents)-Ttod(CrSigChe.Vencs))
EndScan 
Select CrSigChe

Index On Dtos(Vencs)+Bancos+Agencias+nContas+nCheques Tag Venc
Index On Bancos + Agencias + nContas + ncheques Tag Cheque
Index On Agencias + nContas + nCheques Tag Agencia
Index On nContas + ncheques Tag Conta
Index On Ncheques Tag NCheque
Index On Str(Valors,12,2)+ Bancos + Agencias + nContas + ncheques Tag Valor
Index On Numeros Tag Numeros
Index On Nums Tag Nums
Set Order To Venc
Go Bottom
lcNum = CrSigChe.Nums


lcQuery = [Select * From SigCqChm Where Nums = '] + lcNum + [' Order By Nums, Dtsis Desc]
If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, [crSigCqChm]) < 1)
	=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (crSigCqChm)])
	Return .f.
EndIf
Select crSigCqChm
Go Top In crSigCqChm

lcQuery = [Select * From SigChea Where Nums = '] + lcNum + [']
If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, [crSigChea]) < 1)
	=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (crSigChea)])
	Return .f.
EndIf
Select crSigChea
Go Top In crSigChea

Select CrSigChe
If Reccount('CrSigChe') > 0
	With llthispage.GradeCheque
		.RecordSource = "CrSigChe"
		.Column9.ControlSource = "CrSigChe.Emps"
		.Column1.ControlSource = "CrSigChe.Vencs"
		.Column2.ControlSource = "CrSigChe.Bancos"
		.Column3.ControlSource = "CrSigChe.Agencias"
		.Column4.ControlSource = "CrSigChe.ncontas"
		.Column5.ControlSource = "CrSigChe.ncheques"
		.Column6.ControlSource = "CrSigChe.valors"
		.Column7.ControlSource = "CrSigChe.grucarts"
		.Column8.ControlSource = "CrSigChe.concarts"
		.Column10.ControlSource = "CrSigChe.Numeros"
		.Column11.ControlSource = "CrSigChe.DiasP"
		.SetAll('DynamicForeColor','Iif(!IsEmpty(CrSigChe.Tipo),RGB(0,0,255),RGB(0,0,0))','Column')
		.SetAll('DynamicBackColor','Iif(!IsEmpty(CrSigChe.Tipo),RGB(220,255,220),RGB(255,255,255))','Column')
	EndWith

	With llthispage.GradeMovimenta
		.RecordSource = "CrSigCqChm"
		.Column1.ControlSource = "CrSigCqChm.Codigos"
		.Column2.ControlSource = "CrSigCqChm.Nalineas"
		.Column3.ControlSource = "CrSigCqChm.dtents"
		.Column4.ControlSource = "CrSigCqChm.gruorigs"
		.Column5.ControlSource = "CrSigCqChm.conorigs"
		.Column6.ControlSource = "CrSigCqChm.grupos"
		.Column7.ControlSource = "CrSigCqChm.contas"
		.Column8.ControlSource = "CrSigCqChm.Numlotes"
		.Column9.ControlSource = "CrSigCqChm.protocolos"
	EndWith
	
	With llthispage.GradeProrrog
		.RecordSource = "CrSigChea"
		.Column1.ControlSource = "ttod(CrSigChea.Vencs)"
	EndWith

EndIf

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE mretcpf
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE mretcpf

Lparameters lcCpf1
Local lcCpf2

m.lcCpf1 = Iif(Type('m.lcCpf1')=='C',m.lcCpf1,'')
m.lcCpf2 = m.lcCpf1
m.lcCpf1 = Strtran(Strtran(Strtran(m.lcCpf1,".",""),"-",""),"/","")
m.lcCpf1 = Padr(Iif(Empty(m.lcCpf1),Chr(254),m.lcCpf2),20)

Return(m.lcCpf1)

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE pcartao
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE pcartao
Local lcCdConta

lcCdConta = Padr(ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Consulta.Origem.Get_Conta.Value,10)
ThisForm.OkCartao = .T.

Zap In TmpCart

If Used('TmpCard')
	Use In TmpCard
EndIf

lStrQuery = [Select cartaos, datas, fpags, valos, nocreditos, vencs, totres, totpar From SigCdCrd ]+;
			[Where iclis = ']+lcCdConta+[' ]
lnQueryOk = ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lStrQuery, 'TmpCard')
If lnQueryOk < 1
	MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
	Return(.F.)
EndIf
Select TmpCard
Go Top
Scan
	If (TmpCard.totres - TmpCard.totpar) <> 0
		Insert Into TmpCart (cartaos,datas,fpags,valos,nocreditos,vencs) ;
					Values (TmpCard.cartaos,TmpCard.datas,TmpCard.fpags,TmpCard.valos,TmpCard.nocreditos,TmpCard.vencs)
	EndIf
EndScan
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE pestoque
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE pestoque

Local lcCdConta

lcCdConta = Padr( ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Consulta.Origem.Get_Conta.Value, 10 )

ThisForm.OkEstoque = .T.

Zap In dbEstoque

loBarraEmp = CreateObject('fwprogressbar','Verificando Estoque das Empresas...',Reccount('CrSigCdEmp'))
loBarraEmp.Show

lStrQuery = [Select a.grupos, a.cpros, Sum(a.sqtds) as Sqtds From SigMvEst a, SigCdEmp b ]+;
			[Where a.Estos = ']+lcCdconta+[' And a.Emps = b.Cemps And b.Ativas = 1 Group by a.grupos,a.cpros]
lnQueryOk = ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lStrQuery, 'TmpEst')
If lnQueryOk < 1
	MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
	Return(.F.)
EndIf
Select TmpEst
Go Top
Scan
	If TmpEst.sqtds <> 0
		Insert Into dbEstoque (grupos,cpros,sqtds) Values (TmpEst.grupos,TmpEst.cpros,TmpEst.sqtds)
		=ThisForm.Carrega_Combo(TmpEst.grupos,;
			ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Estoque.Cmb_Grupo,'dbEstoque')
	EndIf
EndScan
loBarraEmp.Complete(.T.)

Go Top In dbEstoque
ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Vendas.Grd_Produto.Refresh

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE pproduto
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE pproduto

Local lcCdConta, lnRateio, lnCasas, lnValTots, lnValItens, lcMoedas, lcMes, lcAnos, ldDtIni, ldDtFim
Local lnTotals, lnVrMovs

Private pDti, pDtf

lcCdConta = Padr(ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Consulta.Origem.Get_Conta.Value,10)
ThisForm.OkProduto = .T. 

ldDtIni = ThisForm.fld_principal.pag_resultado.cnt_periodo.get_dt_inicial.Value 
ldDtFim = ThisForm.fld_principal.pag_resultado.cnt_periodo.get_dt_final.Value 

If Not Empty(ldDtIni)
	pDti = fDtoSQL(ldDtIni)
EndIf 

If Not Empty(ldDtFim)
	pDtf = fDtoSQL(ldDtFim,[23:59:59])
EndIf 

Zap In dbOpera
Zap In dbOperaP
Zap In dbConserto
Zap In dbItens
Zap In dbEstIte
Zap In dbProduc
Zap In dbTotVendas
Set Order To MesAnos Ascending In dbTotVendas
Go Top In CrSigCdPam

loBarra = CreateObject( 'fwprogressbar', 'Processando Produtos e Operações. Aguarde...', 9 )
loBarra.Show

* Apanhando as Operações de Subnível
lcQuery = [Select a.Nivels ] + ;
			[From SigCdOpe a ] + ;
		   [Where Not a.Nivels In ('] + crSigCdPam.opconrclis + [', '] + CrSigCdPam.opconrfors + [','] + CrSigCdPam.opconfors + [', Space(20)) And ] + ;
		   		 [(a.BaixaSns = 1 Or a.BxParcials = 1 Or a.BxAutos = 1) ] + ;
		   [Union  ] + ; &&All 
		  [Select b.CNivels as Nivels ] + ;
			[From SigOpCsn b, SigCdOpe c ] + ;
		   [Where b.cDopes = c.Dopes And ] + ;
		   		 [Not b.cNivels In ('] + crSigCdPam.opconrclis + [', '] + CrSigCdPam.opconrfors + [','] + CrSigCdPam.opconfors + [', Space(20)) And ] + ;
		   		 [(c.BaixaSns = 1 Or c.BxParcials = 1 Or c.BxAutos = 1)]

If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, [LocalSubs]) < 1)
	=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (GeraPedidos - LocalSubs)])
	Return .f.
EndIf

Select Distinct Nivels ;
  From LocalSubs ;
  Into Cursor TmpOpe ReadWrite
Select TmpOpe
Index On Nivels Tag Nivels
Go Top In TmpOpe
************************************************

lcPGrupo   = CrSigCdPam.grupoests
lcPConta   = CrSigCdPam.contaests
lcOperacao = CrSigCdPam.dopppads
lcServico  = CrSigCdPam.doppservs
lcOpConsers= crSigCdPam.OpConsers

Wait Window 'Aguarde! Selecionando Movimentações ...' NoWait

lStrQuery = [Select Dopes,Vendas,Valpres,cMoes,Casas,globalizas,Servicos,Produc,Origems,EstOrigs,Destinos,EstDests,CupFis From SigCdOpe ]
If ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lStrQuery, 'CrTmpOpe') < 1
	MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
	Return(.F.)
EndIf

Select CrTmpOpe
Index On Dopes Tag dopes

lStrQuery = [Select '1' as tps, GrupoDs as Grupos,Dopes,ChkSubn,Grupoos,GrupoDs,ContaOs,ContaDs,Datas,]+;
			[Emps,Numes,MascNum,Obses,Chkpagos,Utilizados,EmpDopNums,Vars,PrazoEnts ] +;
			[From SigMvCab Where ContaDs = ']+lcCdConta+[' ]+ ;
			Iif(Not Empty(ldDtIni),[ And Datas >= ?pDti ],[]) + ;
			Iif(Not Empty(ldDtFim),[ And Datas <= ?pDtf ],[]) + ;			
			[Union all ]+;
			[Select '2' as tps, GrupoOs as Grupos,Dopes,ChkSubn,Grupoos,GrupoDs,ContaOs,ContaDs,Datas,]+; 
			[Emps,Numes,MascNum,Obses,Chkpagos,Utilizados,EmpDopNums,Vars,PrazoEnts ] +;
			[From SigMvCab Where ContaOs = ']+lcCdConta+[' And Not ContaDs = ']+lcCdConta+[' ] +;
			Iif(Not Empty(ldDtIni),[ And Datas >= ?pDti ],[]) + ;
			Iif(Not Empty(ldDtFim),[ And Datas <= ?pDtf ],[])
			
If ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lStrQuery, 'CrTprMvCab') < 1
	MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
	Return(.F.)
EndIf

lStrQuery = [Select * From SigOpPed ]
If ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lStrQuery, 'CrSigOpPed') < 1
	MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
	Return(.F.)
EndIf

Select a.* From CrTprMvCab a, TmpOpe b, CrSigOpPed c Where Not a.ChkSubn And a.dopes = b.Nivels And ;
a.Dopes = c.dopes And a.dopes <> lcOpConsers ;
Into Cursor CrConserto2

Select a.*, b.ValPres, b.Cmoes From CrTprMvCab a, CrTmpOpe b where a.Dopes = b.dopes And b.Vendas = 1 ;
Into Cursor CrVendas

Select CrSigTempC
Zap

lcCidQuery   = fUniqueIds()

Select CrVendas
Scan
	Select CrSigTempC
	Append Blank
	Replace EmpDopNums With CrVendas.EmpDopNums,;
			CidQuerys  With lcCidQuery,;
			Grupos     With CrVendas.grupos,;
			CidChaves  With fUniqueIds(),;
			datas      with Crvendas.Datas,;
			mascnum    with crvendas.mascnum,;
			ValPres    with CrVendas.ValPres,;
			cMoes      with crvendas.cmoes,;
			Vars       With CrVendas.Vars
EndScan
If !ThisForm.Podatamgr.UpDate('CrSigTempC')
	MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
	Return .f.
EndIf
If ThisForm.PoDataMgr.commit() < 1
	MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
	Return .f.
Endif

lStrQuery = [Select b.Grupos, c.Cgrus, a.Cpros, a.Dpros, a.Qtds, a.dopes, b.MascNum, b.Datas, ]+;
			[(a.Totas / a.Qtds) as Totas, a.CodBarras, a.Emps, a.Opers, a.Numes, a.Utilizas, ]+;
			[(a.Totas * (1 + (b.Vars / 100)) / a.Qtds) as OrigTotas, ]+;
			[(a.Univals * a.Qtds) as BasTotas, c.Colecoes, a.Pesos, a.Moedas, a.citens, ]+;
			[e.EmpDopNums,e.Lcancelas,e.ChkSubn,e.ProcBals,e.ProcDBal,e.ChkPagos,e.ChkBxParcs,e.Nops,e.Notas, ]+;
			[f.codtams, g.Empos ]+;
			[From SigMvItn a left Join SigMvIts f on f.empdopnums = a.empdopnums and f.citens = a.citens ]+;
			[Left Join SigOpEtq g On g.cbars = a.CodBarras, ]+;
			[SigTempC b, SigCdPro c, SigMvCab E ]+;
			[Where a.EmpDopNums = b.EmpDopNums And b.CidQuerys = ?lcCidQuery And a.Cpros=c.Cpros ]+;
			[And Not (b.ValPres = 3 And Not b.CMoes='   ') And a.EmpDopNums = e.EmpdopNums ]

If ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lStrQuery, 'CrItens') < 1
	MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
	Return(.F.)
EndIf

If ThisForm.poDataMgr.fSqlApagarTmp('SigTempC',lcCIdQuery,'SIGOPCGV') < 1
	MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo !!!',16,'Falha na Conexão - (SigTempC)')
	Return(.F.)
EndIf

Update CrItens Set Datas = Nvl(Datas, Ctod([]))

Select DbProdTodos
Append From Dbf('CrItens')
Scan
	=Seek(DbProdTodos.Dopes,'CrTmpOpe','Dopes')
	Replace DbProdTodos.Dopes 		With CrTmpOpe.Dopes,;
			DbProdTodos.Produc		With CrTmpOpe.Produc,;
			DbProdTodos.Origems		With CrTmpOpe.Origems,;
			DbProdTodos.EstOrigs	With CrTmpOpe.EstOrigs,;
			DbProdTodos.Destinos	With CrTmpOpe.Destinos,;
			DbProdTodos.EstDests	With CrTmpOpe.EstDests,;
			DbProdTodos.CupFis		With CrTmpOpe.CupFis,;
			DbProdTodos.Empos       With Nvl(DbProdTodos.Empos,''),;
			DbProdTodos.CodTams     With Nvl(DbProdTodos.CodTams,'')
EndScan 

Update DbProdTodos; 	
Set A = (DbProdTodos.Produc # 1), ;
    B = ((DbProdTodos.Origems=1 And InList(DbProdTodos.EstOrigs,1,2,4)) Or (DbProdTodos.Destinos=1 And InList(DbProdTodos.EstDests,1,2,4))), ;
    C = (DbProdTodos.ProcBals And DbProdTodos.ProcDBal), ;
    D = (DbProdTodos.Nops>0 And (DbProdTodos.ChkSubn  Or DbProdTodos.ChkBxParcs)),;
    E = (DbProdTodos.ProcBals),;
	F = (DbProdTodos.ProcDBal),;
	G = (DbProdTodos.ChkPagos)

Select * From CrVendas Where ValPres = 3 And Not Empty(Cmoes) Into Cursor CrVendas2

If Not Empty(crSigCdPam.OpConsers)
	Select a.* From CrTprMvCab a, TmpOpe b Where Not a.ChkSubn And a.dopes = b.Nivels And a.Dopes = lcOpConsers;
	Into Cursor CrConserto1 Order by DataS  

	Select CrConserto1

	Scan
		If ContaDs = lcCdConta
			_Grupo = Grupods
		Else
			_Grupo = Grupoos
		EndIf

		_Emp = Emps
		_Num = Numes


		Insert Into DbConserto ( Grupos, Datas, Emps, Dopes, Mascnum, Status, Obscon, Numes, PrazoEnts, ChkPagos, Utilizados ) ;
				Values ( _Grupo, CrConserto1.Datas, CrConserto1.Emps, CrConserto1.Dopes, ;
						 CrConserto1.Mascnum, "Escritório", Padr( Left( CrConserto1.Obses,50 ), 50 ),;
						 CrConserto1.Numes, Nvl(CrConserto1.PrazoEnts,Ctod('')),CrConserto1.ChkPagos, CrConserto1.Utilizados )
		
		lcSql = [Select EmpDopNums From SigMvCab Where EmpDopNums = ']+_Emp + crSigCdPam.OpConRClis + Str( _Num, 6 )+[']
		If Thisform.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcsql,'TmpConserto') < 1
			MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão (Tmpconserto 1)')
			Return(.F.)
		EndIf
		If Reccount('TmpConserto') > 0
			Select DbConserto
			Delete
			_OkConserto = .f.
		Else
			lcSql = [Select EmpDopNums From SigMvCab Where EmpDopNums = ']+_Emp + crSigCdPam.OpConRFors + Str( _Num, 6 )+[']
			If Thisform.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcsql,'TmpConserto') < 1
				MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão (Tmpconserto 2)')
				Return(.F.)
			EndIf
			If Reccount('TmpConserto') > 0
				Replace DbConserto.Status With "Pronto" in DbConserto
				_OkConserto = .t.
			Else
				lcSql = [Select EmpDopNums From SigMvCab Where EmpDopNums = ']+_Emp + crSigCdPam.OpConFors + Str( _Num, 6 )+[']
				If Thisform.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcsql,'TmpConserto') < 1
					MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão (Tmpconserto 3)')
					Return(.F.)
				EndIf
				If Reccount('TmpConserto') > 0
					Replace DbConserto.Status With "Fornecedor" in DbConserto
					_OkConserto = .t.
				Else
					_OkConserto = .t.
				EndIf
			EndIf
		Endif
		If _OkConserto
			=Seek(CrConserto1.Dopes,'CrTmpOpe','Dopes')
			=ThisForm.Carrega_Combo( _Grupo, ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Conserto.Cmb_Grupo, 'DbOpera' ,.F.,.T. )
			=ThisForm.Carrega_Combo( _Grupo, ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Producao.Cmb_Grupo, 'DbOpera' )

			ThisForm.poDataMgr.CursorQuery([SigOpPed], [CrSigOpPed], [Dopes], CrConserto1.Dopes, [Tpagos,Tutils])

			If Not Used([CrSigOpPed])
				=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (CursorQuery - CrSigOpPed)])
				Return 0
			EndIf
			If !Eof('CrSigOpPed')
				Select * from DbOpera  WHERE Grupos = _Grupo and Dopes = CrConserto1.Dopes into Cursor Vopera
				If Reccount('Vopera') = 0
					Insert Into DbOpera ( Grupos, Dopes ) Values ( _Grupo, CrConserto1.dopes)
					If Eof('dbOperaP')
						Insert Into DbOperaP( Grupos, Dopes ) Values ('|', 'TODAS OPERAÇÕES')
					EndIf 
					Insert Into DbOperaP( Grupos, Dopes ) Values ( _Grupo, CrConserto1.dopes)
				EndIf
				Use in Vopera
			EndIf 
		EndIf
	EndScan
EndIf

Select CrConserto2
Scan
	If ContaDs = lcCdConta
		_Grupo = Grupods
	Else
		_Grupo = Grupoos
	EndIf
	
	Insert Into DbConserto ( Grupos, Datas, Emps, Dopes, Mascnum, Status, ;
							 Obscon, Numes, PrazoEnts, ChkPagos, Utilizados ) ;
			Values ( _Grupo, Nvl(CrConserto2.Datas, Ctod('')), CrConserto2.Emps, CrConserto2.Dopes, ;
					 CrConserto2.Mascnum, "Pendente", ;
					 Padr( Left( Nvl(CrConserto2.Obses, ''), 50 ), 50 ), CrConserto2.Numes, ;
					 Nvl(CrConserto2.PrazoEnts, Ctod('')) ,CrConserto2.ChkPagos, CrConserto2.Utilizados )
					 
	=Seek(CrConserto2.Dopes,'CrTmpOpe','Dopes')

	=ThisForm.Carrega_Combo( _Grupo, ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Conserto.Cmb_Grupo, 'DbOpera' ,.F.,.T. )
	=ThisForm.Carrega_Combo( _Grupo, ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Producao.Cmb_Grupo, 'DbOpera' )
	
	ThisForm.poDataMgr.CursorQuery([SigOpPed], [CrSigOpPed], [Dopes], CrConserto2.Dopes, [Tpagos,Tutils])

	If Not Used([CrSigOpPed])
		=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (CursorQuery - CrSigOpPed)])
		Return 0
	EndIf

	If !Eof('CrSigOpPed')
		Select * from DbOpera  WHERE Grupos = _Grupo and Dopes = CrConserto2.Dopes into Cursor Vopera
		If Reccount('Vopera') = 0
			Insert Into DbOpera ( Grupos, Dopes ) Values ( _Grupo, CrConserto2.dopes)
			If Eof('dbOperaP')
				Insert Into DbOperaP( Grupos, Dopes ) Values ('|', 'TODAS OPERAÇÕES')
			EndIf 			
			Insert Into DbOperaP( Grupos, Dopes ) Values ( _Grupo, CrConserto2.dopes)			
		EndIf
		Use in Vopera		
	EndIf 
	llSemDados = .f.
EndScan

Select CrVendas2
Scan
	If ContaDs = lcCdConta
		_Campos = "Contads"
		_Grupo = Grupods
	Else
		_Campos = "Contaos"
		_Grupo = Grupoos
	EndIf

	=Seek(CrVendas2.Dopes,'CrTmpOpe','Dopes')

	lnRateio = 1
	lnCasas  = CrTmpOpe.Casas
	lnValTots  = 0
	lnValItens = 0
	
	lcSql = [Select * From SigMvMov Where EmpDopNums = ']+CrVendas2.Emps + CrVendas2.Dopes + Str(CrVendas2.Numes,6)+[']
	If ThisForm.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcsql,'CrTmpEstMo') < 1
		MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão (CrTmpEstMo)')
		Return(.F.)
	EndIf
	Select CrTmpEstMo
	Scan 
		If CrTmpEstMo.Moeds = CrTmpOpe.Cmoes
			lnValTots  = lnValTots + CrTmpEstMo.ValTots
		EndIf
	EndScan
	
	lcSql = [Select a.*, b.Colecoes, b.Cgrus  From SigMvItn a Left Join SigCdPro b On a.cpros = b.cpros ]+;
			[Where a.EmpDopNums = ']+CrVendas2.Emps + CrVendas2.Dopes + Str(CrVendas2.Numes,6)+[']
	If ThisForm.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcsql,'CrTprMvCabi') < 1
		MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão (CrTprMvCabi)')
		Return(.F.)
	EndIf
	Select CrTprMvCabi
	Scan
		If CrTprMvCabi.Opers = "E"
			lnValTots  = lnValTots + CrTprMvCabi.Totas
		Else
			If Not IsEmpty(CrTprMvCabi.Notas) AND IsEmpty(CrTprMvCabi.Codbarras)
				lnValTots  = lnValTots - Iif(CrTprMvCabi.Opers='S',1,-1) * CrTprMvCabi.Totas
			Else
				lnValItens  = lnValItens + Iif(CrTprMvCabi.Opers='S',1,-1) * CrTprMvCabi.Totas
			EndIf
		Endif
	EndScan
	If lnValTots # 0 And lnValItens # 0
		lnRateio = Abs( lnValTots / lnValItens )
	EndIf

	Select CrTprMvCabi
	Scan
		Insert Into DbProdTodos ( Grupos, Cgrus, Cpros, Dpros, Qtds, Dopes, ;
				MascNum, Datas, Totas, CodBarras, Emps, Opers, Numes, Utilizas, ;
				OrigTotas, BasTotas, Colecoes, Pesos, Moedas,citens) ;
			Values ( _Grupo, CrTprMvCabi.CGrus, CrTprMvCabi.Cpros, CrTprMvCabi.Dpros, ;
				CrTprMvCabi.Qtds, crTprMvCabi.Dopes, CrVendas2.MascNum, CrVendas2.Datas, ;
				Round( ( crTprMvCabi.Totas * Iif( Not IsEmpty(crTprMvCabi.Notas) AND IsEmpty(crTprMvCabi.CodBarras), 1, lnRateio) ), lnCasas), ;
				crTprMvCabi.CodBarras, crTprMvCabi.Emps, crTprMvCabi.Opers, crTprMvCabi.Numes, crTprMvCabi.Utilizas, ;
				crTprMvCabi.Totas, crTprMvCabi.Univals, CrTprMvCabi.Colecoes, crTprMvCabi.Pesos, crTprMvCabi.Moedas,CrTprMvCabi.citens)
	EndScan
EndScan

Select Distinct Grupos From DbProdTodos Into Cursor crGrupos

Select Distinct Cgrus From DbProdTodos Into Cursor crcGrus

llTru = .t.
Select CrGrupos
Scan
	=ThisForm.Carrega_Combo( CrGrupos.Grupos, ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Vendas.Cmb_Grupo, .f., llTru)
	llTru = .f.
EndScan
ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Vendas.Cmb_Grupo.Value = Space(10) + ' - Todos'

lcSql = [Select Cgrus, Dgrus From SigCdGrp ]
If ThisForm.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcsql,'CrTmpGru') < 1
	MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão (CrTmpGru)')
	Return(.F.)
EndIf
Select CrTmpGru
Index on Cgrus Tag Cgrus

Select CrCgrus
Scan
	=Seek(CrCgrus.Cgrus,'CrTmpGru','Cgrus')
	With ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Vendas.Cmb_GrProd
		_Inclui = .t.
		For _Cont = 1 to .ListCount
			If .List( _Cont ) = CrTmpGru.Dgrus
				_Inclui = .f.
				Exit
			Endif
		Next
		If _Inclui
			.additem( CrTmpGru.Cgrus + ' - ' + CrTmpGru.Dgrus )
		Endif
	Endwith
EndScan

Select DbConserto
Scan 
	lctEmpDopNums = DbConserto.Emps + DbConserto.Dopes + Str(DbConserto.Numes,6)
	lcQuery = [ Select Emps,Dopes,Numes,Qtds,Qtbaixas,Units,Valrats,Cpros,DPros ] + ;
			  [ From SigMvItn ] + ;
			  [ Where EmpDopNums = '] + lctEmpDopNums + ['] 

	If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, [crTprMvCabi]) < 1)
		=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (crTprMvCabi)])
		Return 0
	EndIf
	
	If Reccount('CrTprMvCabI') = 0
		lcQuery = [ Select Valos ] + ;
				  [ From SigMvCab ] + ;
				  [ Where EmpDopNums = '] + lctEmpDopNums + ['] 

		If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, [LocalEest]) < 1)
			=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (LocalEest)])
			Return 0
		EndIf
		Replace ValPends With LocalEest.Valos In DbConserto
	Else
		Select crTprMvCabi
		Scan
			Replace DbConserto.ValPends with DbConserto.ValPends + 	((Qtds-Qtbaixas) * (Units+Valrats))
		EndScan 
	Endif
EndScan 

Use In TmpOpe
Set Order to MesAnos Descending In dbTotVendas
Go Top In DbTotVendas
Go Top In DbProduto
Go Top In DbProdTodos
Go Top In DbOpera
Go Top In DbOperaP
Go Top In DbConserto
Go Top In DbEstIte

*-[ Insere os Itens de Producao ]-*
Select Distinct Emps, Dopes, Numes ;
  From DbConserto ;
  Into Cursor csCabec
Index On Emps + Dopes + Str(Numes,6) Tag EmpDopNum

Select CrSigTempC
Zap

Select CsCabec
Scan
	Select CrSigTempC
	Append Blank
	Replace EmpDopNums With CsCabec.Emps + CsCabec.Dopes + Str(CsCabec.Numes,6),;
			CidQuerys  With lcCidQuery,;
			CidChaves  With fUniqueIds()
EndScan
If !ThisForm.Podatamgr.UpDate('CrSigTempC')
	MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
	Return .f.
EndIf
If ThisForm.PoDataMgr.commit() < 1
	MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
	Return .f.
EndIf

*-[ Itens ]-*
loBarra.SubTitulo.Caption = "Selecionando Itens de Operações"

Select CrSigTempC
If Reccount() > 0

	lStrQuery = [Select a.emps, a.dopes, a.numes, a.cpros, a.DPros, sum(a.qtds) as qtds, sum(a.qtds-a.qtbaixas) as saldo ]+;
				[From SigMvItn a, SigTempC b ]+;
				[Where a.EmpDopNums = b.EmpDopNums and b.CidQuerys = ?lcCidQuery ] + ;
				[Group By a.emps, a.dopes, a.numes, a.cpros, a.dpros ] 

	If ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lStrQuery, 'CsEestiTmp') < 1
		MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
		Return(.F.)
	EndIf

	If ThisForm.poDataMgr.fSqlApagarTmp('SigTempC',lcCIdQuery,'SIGOPCGV') < 1
		MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo !!!',16,'Falha na Conexão - (SigTempC)')
		Return(.F.)
	EndIf

	Select dbItens
	Append from dbf('csEestiTmp')
	Use in csEestiTmp

Endif

loBarra.Update( .t.,'')

*-[ Estoque ]-*
loBarra.SubTitulo.Caption = "Selecionando Estoque"
Select CrSigTempC
Zap

Select dbItens
Scan
	Select CrSigTempC
	Append Blank
	Replace EmpGruEsts With DbItens.emps + lcpGrupo + lcpConta,;
			Cpros      with Dbitens.Cpros,;
			CidQuerys  With lcCidQuery,;
			CidChaves  With fUniqueIds()
EndScan
If !ThisForm.Podatamgr.UpDate('CrSigTempC')
	MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
	Return .f.
EndIf
If ThisForm.PoDataMgr.commit() < 1
	MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
	Return .f.
EndIf

Select CrSigTempC
If Reccount() > 0

	lStrQuery = [Select a.emps, a.grupos, a.estos, a.cpros, sum(a.sqtds) as sqtds From SigMvEst a, SigTempC b ]+;
				[Where b.EmpGruEsts = a.EmpGruEsts And b.cPros = a.cPros and b.CidQuerys = ?lcCidQuery ] + ;
				[Group By a.Emps, a.grupos, a.estos, a.cPros ]
	If ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lStrQuery, 'CsEstTmp') < 1
		MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
		Return(.F.)
	EndIf

	If ThisForm.poDataMgr.fSqlApagarTmp('SigTempC',lcCIdQuery,'SIGOPCGV') < 1
		MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo !!!',16,'Falha na Conexão - (SigTempC)')
		Return(.F.)
	EndIf

	Select dbEstIte
	Append from dbf('csEstTmp')
	Use in csEstTmp

EndIf

loBarra.Update( .t.,'')

Select csCabec
Scan  
	loBarra.SubTitulo.Caption = "Verificando : "+csCabec.Emps+[-]+alltrim(csCabec.Dopes)+[-]+alltrim(str(csCabec.Numes,6))

	lStrQuery = [Select emps, dopes, numes, nops, empds, qtds, dataes From SigOpPic ]+;
				[Where emps = ']+CsCabec.emps+[' And dopes = "]+CsCabec.dopes+[" And numes = ]+Str(CsCabec.numes,6)+[ ]
	lnQueryOk = ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lStrQuery, 'crTmpOpi')
	If lnQueryOk < 1
		MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
		Return(.F.)
	EndIf
	
	Select CrTmpOpi
	Scan
		lStrQuery = [Select emps, nops, Nenvs, datas, Codpds, grupods, contads From SigPdMvf Where nops = ]+Str(crTmpOpi.nops,10)+[ ]
		lnQueryOk = ThisForm.podataMgr.SqlExecute(lStrQuery, 'crTmpMFas')
		If lnQueryOk < 1
			MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
			Return(.F.)
		EndIf
		Select CrTmpMFas
		Index On Datas Tag Datas Descending
		Go Top
		Scan
			llPula = .t.
			lnOp = crtmpMFas.Nops

			lStrQuery = [Select empdnps, chksubn From SigCdNec ]+;
						[Where EmpDnPs = ']+crTmpMFas.emps+lcOperacao+Str(crTmpMFas.nops,10)+[' ]
			lnQueryOk = ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lStrQuery, 'crTmpNens')
			If lnQueryOk < 1
				MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
				Return(.F.)
			EndIf
			
			Select CrtmpNens
			If !CrTmpNens.ChkSubn And CrTmpMfas.Emps = _Empr
				llPula = .f.
			EndIf
			
			If Not llPula
				lStrQuery = [Select empdnps, chksubn From SigCdNec ]+;
							[Where EmpDnPs = ']+crTmpMFas.emps+lcServico+Str(crTmpMFas.nops,10)+[' ]
				lnQueryOk = ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lStrQuery, 'crTmpNens')
				If lnQueryOk < 1
					MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
					Return(.F.)
				EndIf
				Select CrTmpNens
				If !ChkSubn And CrTmpMfas.Emps = _Empr
					llPula = .f.
				EndIf
			EndIf
			If llPula
				Exit
			EndIf

			Select CrTmpMFas
			lcOp  = Transf(Val( Left(Str(crtmpMFas.Nops,10),6)),'@L 999999')
			lcSeq = Transf(Val(Right(Str(crtmpMFas.Nops,10),4)),'@L 9999'  )
			=Seek(crtmpOpi.Dopes,'crtmpOpe','Dopes')
			lcEdn = Iif(Empty(crTmpOpi.Empds),CrTmpOpi.Emps,CrTmpOpi.Empds)
			lcSql = [Select GrupoOs, ContaOs, GrupoDs, ContaDs From SigMvCab where EmpDopNums = ']+lcEDN+[']
			If ThisForm.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcsql,'CrTprMvCab') < 1
				MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
				Return(.F.)
			Endif
			If crtmpOpe.Globalizas = 1 Or crtmpOpe.Servicos = 1
				lcGrupo = CrTprMvCab.Grupoos
				lcConta = CrTprMvCab.Contaos
			Else
				lcGrupo = crTprMvCab.Grupods
				lcConta = crTprMvCab.Contads
			Endif
			If CrTmpOpi.Qtds # 0
				Insert Into dbProduc ( Op, Sequencia, CPros, Qtde, Fase, Data, Dopes, Numes, emps, Prevs, Nops, Nenvs ) ;
					Values ( lcOp, lcSeq, CrTmpmfas.CodPds, CrTmpOpi.Qtds,;
							crtmpMFas.Grupods, crtmpMfas.Datas, crtmpOpi.Dopes, crtmpOpi.Numes, crtmpOpi.emps, crtmpOpi.Dataes, crtmpOpi.Nops, CrTmpMfas.nenvs)
			EndIf
			
			exit

		EndScan
	EndScan
EndScan

loBarra.SubTitulo.Caption = "Aguarde..."
loBarra.Update( .t.,'')
Use in csCabec

*!*	Select * from dbProduc group by emps, dopes, numes, nops, fase into Cursor dbProd1

Select Emps, Dopes, Numes, Nops, Fase, Max(Op) as Op, Max(Sequencia) as Sequencia, Max(Qtde) as Qtde, ;
	   Max(Cpros) as Cpros, Max(Data) as Data, Max(Prevs) as Prevs, Max(XPer) as XPer, Max(Nenvs) as Nenvs ;
  From dbProduc ;
 Group By Emps, Dopes, Numes, Nops, Fase ;
  Into Cursor dbProd1

Select dbProduc
zap
Append from dbf('dbProd1')
Go top

*-[ Acerta o campo de porcentagem ]-*
Select dbItens
Scan
	Replace dbItens.xPer With round(ABS((dbItens.Saldo/dbItens.qtds)*100),0)
EndScan
loBarra.Update( .t.,'')

Select dbEstIte
Scan
	if Seek(dbEstIte.emps+dbEstIte.cPros,"dbItens","cPros") and dbItens.saldo <> 0
		Replace dbEstIte.xper with round(ABS((dbEstIte.sqtds/dbItens.saldo)*100),0)
	endif
EndScan
loBarra.Update( .t.,'')
Go Top

Select dbConserto
Go Top

Select dbProduc
Scan
	if seek(dbProduc.Emps+dbProduc.Dopes+str(dbProduc.Numes,6)+dbProduc.Cpros,"dbItens","EmpDopNum") and dbItens.saldo <>0
		Replace dbProduc.xper With round(ABS((dbProduc.Qtde/dbItens.saldo)*100),0)
	endif					
EndScan
loBarra.Update( .t.,'')
loBarra.Complete(.t.)

Select dbProduc
Set Order to EmpDopNum
Set key to dbConserto.emps+dbConserto.dopes+str(dbConserto.numes,6)

Go Top In dbProduc
Go Top In dbItens
Go Top In DbOpera
Go Top In DbOperaP

With ThisForm.fld_principal.pag_resultado.Result
	.Vendas.Grd_Produto.Refresh
	.Conserto.grd_Conserto.AfterRowColChange
	.Conserto.grd_Opera.Refresh
	With .Producao
		.Grd_Conserto.Refresh
		.Grd_Opera.Refresh
		.Grd_Itens.Refresh
		.Grd_Produc.Refresh
		.Grd_Estoque.Refresh
	EndWith
EndWith	

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE processa
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE processa
Local lcCdConta, llSemDados, _TotAbe, _TotDev, _Valos, lcArquivo1

Private lcDia, ldDtIni, ldDtFin

lcArquivo1 = Sys(2023) + '\' + Sys(2015) + '.Jpg'

*-[ Caso a consulta já esteja executada, não processa novamente ]-*
If !ThisForm.ConsultaExecutada 
	Wait Window 'Aguarde! Processando...' NoWait

	pcMensagem = .F.
	ThisForm.ResultPageToSet = 1
	
	With ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result
		.Pendencias.Cmb_Grupo.Clear
		.Cheque.Cmb_Grupo.Clear
		.Vendas.Cmb_Grupo.Clear
		.Estoque.Cmb_Grupo.Clear
		.Saldo.Cmb_Grupo.Clear
		.Conserto.Cmb_Grupo.Clear
		.Producao.Cmb_Grupo.Clear

		.Vendas.Cmb_GrProd.Clear
		.Vendas.Cmb_GrProd.AddItem('    - Todos os Grupos')
		.Vendas.Cmb_GrProd.Value = '    - Todos os Grupos'
	EndWith

	lcCdConta = Padr(ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Consulta.Origem.Get_Conta.Value,10)
	If Not ThisForm.poDataMgr.CursorQuery('SigCdCli','crTmpCli','iclis',lcCdConta,'Grupos,Iclis','Iclis')
		MessageBox('Conta Inválida !!!',0+48,'')
		Return(.F.)
	EndIf

	With ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Saldo.Gradei
		.RecordSource          = ''
	EndWith

	Select crTmpCli
	Locate 

&&	ThisForm.pcEscolha = 'CONSULTAR'

	If Not ThisForm.fld_principal.pag_resultado.cntConta.mLeDados(crTmpCli.Grupos, crTmpCli.Iclis, '0')
		MessageBox('Erro Na Leitura dos Dados da Conta '+Alltrim(lcCdConta)+'.',0+48,'')
		Return(.F.)
	EndIf 
	
	USE in crTmpCli

	With ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Saldo.Gradei
		.RecordSource          = 'CrSigClLcr'
		.Column1.ControlSource = 'CrSigClLcr.grupos'
		.Column2.ControlSource = 'CrSigClLcr.limcres'
		.Column3.ControlSource = 'CrSigClLcr.moedas'
	EndWith

	With ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result
		With .vendas
			.fweditdata_UltComp.Value = Nvl(crSigCdCli.ultcomps,Ctod(Space(8)))
			.Refresh
		EndWith
	EndWith

	llSemDados = .T.
	
	*-[ Limpa os Arquivos ]-*
	Zap In dbProdTodos
	Zap In dbProduto
	Zap In TmpPar
	Zap In dbHistorico
	Zap In dbDevolvido
	Zap In dbAberto 
	Zap In dbSaldo
	Zap In DbFollow
	Zap In DbTitulo
	Zap In crTmpTipos

	ThisForm.Fld_principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Pendencias.getTotPends.Value = 0

	Wait Window 'Aguarde! Verificando Históricos...' NoWait

	ldDataIni = ''
	ldDataFin = ''
	If !Empty(thisform.fld_principal.pag_resultado.cnt_periodo.get_dt_inicial.Value) and !Empty(thisform.fld_principal.pag_resultado.cnt_periodo.get_dt_final.Value)
		ldDataIni = fDtoSQL(thisform.fld_principal.pag_resultado.cnt_periodo.get_dt_inicial.Value)
		ldDataFin = fDtoSQL(thisform.fld_principal.pag_resultado.cnt_periodo.get_dt_final.Value,'23:59:59')
	EndIf
	If Used('TmpMccr')
		Use In TmpMccr
	EndIf
	lStrQuery = [Select * From SigMvCcr ]+;
				[Where contas = ']+lcCdConta+[' ] +;
				Iif(Empty(ldDataIni) or Empty(ldDataFin),[],[and Datas between ?ldDataIni and ?ldDataFin])
	If ThisForm.podataMgr.SqlExecute(lStrQuery, 'TmpMccr') < 1
		MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo !!!',16,'Falha na Conexão - (TmpMccr)')
		Return(.F.)
	EndIf

	Select TmpMccr 
	Scan
		Insert Into DbHistorico (Emps, Grupos, Datas, Valors, Opers, Saldos, Hists, Hist2s, Moedas, Dopes, Numes) ;
				Values (TmpMccr.Emps, TmpMccr.Grupos, Nvl(TmpMccr.Datas,Ctod(Space(08))), TmpMccr.Valors, ;
						TmpMccr.Opers, TmpMccr.Saldos, TmpMccr.Hists, TmpMccr.Hist2s, TmpMccr.Moedas, ;
						TmpMccr.Dopes, TmpMccr.Numes)
		llSemDados = .f.
		
		If Nopers = 0 Or !Inlist( Tipos, ' ', 'X' ) Or (!Empty( Dopes ) And !Empty( Numes ) And Tipos # 'X')
			Loop
		Endif

		_Valos = ( TmpMccr.Valors - TmpMccr.Valpags )
		If _Valos > 0 
			Insert Into TmpPar ( Emps, Grupos, Vencs, Fpags, Valos, Moefpgs, Hists, Opers ) ;
					Values ( TmpMccr.Emps, TmpMccr.Grupos, Nvl(TmpMccr.Vencs,Ctod(Space(08))), 'C/Corrente',;
							 _Valos, TmpMccr.Moedas, TmpMccr.Hists, TmpMccr.Opers )
			=ThisForm.Carrega_Combo( TmpMccr.Grupos, ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Pendencias.Cmb_Grupo, 'TmpPar' )
		EndIf 
	EndScan

	If ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Pendencias.Cmb_Grupo.ListCount = 0
		Zap In TmpPar	
	EndIf 

	Wait Window 'Aguarde! Verificando Saldo...' NoWait
	If Empty(thisform.fld_principal.pag_resultado.cnt_periodo.get_dt_inicial.Value) or Empty(thisform.fld_principal.pag_resultado.cnt_periodo.get_dt_final.Value)
		If Used('TmpSccr')
			Use In TmpSccr
		EndIf
		*lStrQuery = [Select grupos, moedas, saldos From SigMvSlc Where contas = ']+lcCdConta+[' Order By grupos,contas ]
		
		lStrQuery = [Select a.grupos, c.descrs, a.contas, b.rclis, a.moedas, a.saldos, isnull(d.LimCres,0) as LimCres, ] + ;
						[Case When isnull(d.LimCres,0) > 0 Then (isnull(d.LimCres,0) - a.saldos) else a.saldos end as SaldoConta ] + ;
						[from SigMvSlc a ] + ; 
						[join sigcdcli b on a.contas = b.iclis ] + ;
						[join sigcdgcr c on a.grupos = c.codigos ] + ;
						[left join sigClLcr d on a.contas = d.contas ] + ;
						[where a.contas = ']+lcCdConta+[' ] + ;
						[order by a.grupos, a.contas ]		
		If ThisForm.podataMgr.SqlExecute(lStrQuery, 'TmpSccr') < 1
			MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo !!!',16,'Falha na Conexão - (TmpSccr)')
			Return(.F.)
		EndIf
		Select TmpSccr
		Go Top

		Scan
			Insert Into dbSaldo (grupos,moedas,saldos,SaldoConta) Values (TmpSccr.grupos,TmpSccr.moedas,TmpSccr.saldos,TmpSccr.SaldoConta)
			llSemDados = .F.
			=ThisForm.Carrega_Combo(TmpSccr.grupos,;
				ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Saldo.Cmb_Grupo,'dbSaldo')
		EndScan 
		Go Top In dbSaldo
	Else
		Select grupos, moedas, Sum(Valors * Iif(opers=[D],1,-1)) as saldos ;
		from DbHistorico group by grupos, moedas into cursor TmpSccr

		Select TmpSccr
		Scan
			Insert Into dbSaldo (grupos,moedas,saldos) Values (TmpSccr.grupos,TmpSccr.moedas,TmpSccr.saldos)
			llSemDados = .F.
			=ThisForm.Carrega_Combo(TmpSccr.grupos,;
				ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Saldo.Cmb_Grupo,'dbSaldo')
		EndScan 
		Go Top In dbSaldo	
	EndIf

	If ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Saldo.Cmb_Grupo.ListCount = 0
		Zap In dbSaldo	
	EndIf 

	Wait Window 'Aguarde! Verificando Históricos (Origem/Destino)...' NoWait
	If Used('TprMvCab')
		Use In TprMvCab
	EndIf
	
	=ThisForm.Podatamgr.cursorquery('SigCdOpe','CrTmpOpe',.f.,.f.,[copers, dopes, valpends, parcs])
	=ThisForm.Podatamgr.cursorquery('SigOpFp','CrTmpFpag',.f.,.f.,[Infos, fpags, valpends, formas, liscongv])

	lStrQuery = [Select a.Grupods, a.Contads, a.GrupoOs, a.ContaOs, a.EmpdopNums, c.emps, c.dopes, c.numes, ]+;
				[c.vencs, c.fpags, c.moefpgs, c.bancos, c.agencias, c.contas, c.numeros, c.Valos, c.Vpags  ]+;
				[From SigMvCab a, SigMvPar c Where a.Contads = ']+lcCdConta+[' ]+;
				[And a.EmpDopNums = c.EmpDopNums And c.pagos='1' ]+;
				Iif(Empty(ldDataIni) or Empty(ldDataFin),[],[and a.Datas between ?ldDataIni and ?ldDataFin ]) +;
				[Union All ]+;
	 			[Select a.Grupods, a.Contads, a.GrupoOs, a.ContaOs, a.EmpdopNums, c.emps, c.dopes, c.numes, ]+;
				[c.vencs, c.fpags, c.moefpgs, c.bancos, c.agencias, c.contas, c.numeros, c.Valos, c.Vpags ]+;
				[From SigMvCab a, SigMvPar c Where a.Contaos = ']+lcCdConta+[' ]+;
				[And Not a.Contads = ']+lcCdConta+[' And a.EmpDopNums = c.EmpDopNums And c.pagos='1'  ] +;
				Iif(Empty(ldDataIni) or Empty(ldDataFin),[],[and a.Datas between ?ldDataIni and ?ldDataFin ])
				
	If ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lStrQuery,'crTmpPar2') < 1
		MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
		Return(.F.)
	EndIf

	Select a.*, b.COpers, d.infos from CrTmpPar2 a, CrtmpOpe b, CrTmpFpag d ;
	Where a.Dopes = b.Dopes And b.ValPends = 1 And b.Parcs = 1 and a.Fpags = d.Fpags And d.ValPends = 1 And d.LisConGv <> 2;
	Into Cursor CrTmpPar

	Select crTmpPar
	Scan
		If cOpers = 1
			_Grupo = Grupods
			If ContaDs <> lcCdConta
				Loop
			Endif
		Else
			_Grupo = Grupoos
			If ContaOs <> lcCdConta
				Loop
			Endif
		EndIf
		
		Scatter Memvar 
		If m.Infos = "C"
			lStrQuery = [Select umovs From SigChe ]+;
						[Where bancos = ']+m.bancos+[' And agencias = ']+m.agencias+[' ]+;
							[And ncontas = ']+m.contas+[' And ncheques = ']+m.numeros+[' ]
			If ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lStrQuery, 'TmpChe') < 1
				MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
				Return(.F.)
			EndIf
			=Seek( TmpChe.UMovs, "crSigCdOpt", "Operacaos" ) 
			If CrSigCdOpt.Tipos # "DB"
				Loop
			Endif
		Endif
		_Valos = Valos - Vpags
		If _Valos > 0
			Insert Into TmpPar ( Emps, Grupos, Vencs, Fpags, Valos, Moefpgs, ;
								 Hists, Opers, Dopes, Numes ) ;
					Values ( m.Emps, _Grupo, m.Vencs, m.Fpags, ;
							 _Valos, m.Moefpgs, m.Emps + ;
							 Alltrim( m.Dopes ) + ' - ' + ;
							 Alltrim( fGerMascara( m.Numes ) ), ;
							 Iif( m.COpers = 1, 'D', 'C' ), ;
							 m.Dopes, m.Numes )
			=ThisForm.Carrega_Combo( _Grupo, ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Pendencias.Cmb_Grupo, 'TmpPar' )
		Endif
	EndScan
	** Monta grade dos tipos para escolher filtro
	If Used([crTiposAux])
		Use in crTiposAux
	Endif	
	Select Distinct(fPags) as fPags from TmpPar into cursor crTiposAux
	Select crTiposAux
	Scan
		Insert into crTmpTipos Values(1,crTiposAux.fPags)
	EndScan
	ThisForm.fld_principal.Pag_resultado.Result.Pendencias.grdTipos.Refresh()     
	**
		
	Wait Clear
	
	*-[ Apanha os Dados Para a Página de Operações ]-*
	With ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Vendas
		If Used('CsOpeUsadas')
			Use In CsOpeUsadas
		EndIf
		lStrQuery = [Select Distinct dopes From SigMvCab Where (contads = ']+lcCdConta+[' Or ContaOs = ']+lcCdConta+[') And ]+;
					[Dopes Not in ('PAGAMENTO','RECEBIMENTO') ] +;
					Iif(Empty(ldDataIni) or Empty(ldDataFin),[],[and Datas between ?ldDataIni and ?ldDataFin])					
		lnQueryOk = ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lStrQuery, 'CsOpeUsadas')
		If lnQueryOk < 1
			MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
			Return(.F.)
		EndIf
		
		*-[ Inicializa o combo de operações do cliente ]-*
		lcVal = ''
		With .CmbOperacoes
			.Clear
			Select CsOpeUsadas
			Go Top
			lcVal = CsOpeUsadas.dopes
			Scan
				.AddItem(CsOpeUsadas.dopes)
			EndScan
			.Value = lcVal
		EndWith
	EndWith
	Go Top In dbHistorico

	_TotAbe  = 0
	_TotDev  = 0
	_Grupo   = CrSigCdPam.grpadclis
	_Conta   = lcCdConta
	_Limite  = 0
	_MoeLim  = CrSigCdPam.moedaps
	_ChequeC = 0
	_ChequeP = 0
	_ChqAv   = 0
	_ChqVc   = 0
	_ChqDv   = 0
	_ChqNeg  = 0
	_Saldo   = 0
	_Disp    = 0
	_VlEstq  = 0

	If Used('TmpChe')
		Use In TmpChe
	EndIf
	lStrQuery = [Select a.*, b.Devolvidos From SigChe a, SigCqChm c, SigCdOpt b ]+;
				[Where a.iclis = ']+lcCdConta+[' And a.Umovs = b.Operacaos And a.Nums = c.Nums ]+;
				[And b.tipos = 'DB' And b.Congvs <> 'N' ] +;
				Iif(Empty(ldDataIni) or Empty(ldDataFin),[],[and a.Datas between ?ldDataIni and ?ldDataFin])					

	If ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lStrQuery, 'TmpChe') < 1
		MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
		Return(.F.)
	EndIf
	Select TmpChe
	Scan
		Insert Into DbAberto ( Grupos, Vencs, Bancos, Agencias, NContas, NCheques, Valors, Nums, Devolvidos ) ;
					Values ( crSigCdPam.GrPadClis, TmpChe.Vencs, TmpChe.Bancos, TmpChe.Agencias,;
							TmpChe.NContas, TmpChe.NCheques, TmpChe.Valors, TmpChe.Nums, TmpChe.Devolvidos )
		=ThisForm.Carrega_Combo( crSigCdPam.GrPadClis, ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Cheque.Cmb_Grupo, 'DbAberto' )
		llSemDados = .f.
		If ThisForm.PodataMgr.Cursorquery('SigCheh','TmpCheh','Nums',TmpChe.Nums)
			Select TmpCheH
			Scan
				Replace DbAberto.Flags With .t., DbAberto.Nums With TmpChe.Nums In DbAberto
				Insert Into DbFollow (Nums, Datas, Hists ) Values ;
					(TmpCheh.Nums, TmpCheh.Datas, TmpCheh.Hists)
			EndScan
		EndIf
	Endscan

	If Used('TmpDevolvidos')
		Use In TmpDevolvidos
	EndIf

	lStrQuery = [Select a.vencs,a.bancos,a.agencias,a.ncontas,a.ncheques,a.valors,a.nums,a.grclis,a.grclis As grupos ]+;
				[From SigChe a, SigCqChm b, SigCdOpt c ]+;
				[Where a.iclis = ']+lcCdConta+[' And a.nums = b.nums And b.codigos = c.operacaos And c.devolvidos = "S" ] +;
				Iif(Empty(ldDataIni) or Empty(ldDataFin),[],[and a.Datas between ?ldDataIni and ?ldDataFin])
	lnQueryOk = ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lStrQuery, 'TmpDevolvidos')
	If lnQueryOk < 1
		MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
		Return(.F.)
	EndIf

	Select TmpDevolvidos
	Go Top
	Scan
		=ThisForm.Carrega_Combo(TmpDevolvidos.grclis,;
			ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Cheque.Cmb_Grupo,'dbDevolvido')
		llSemDados = .F.

		lcCheque = TmpDevolvidos.bancos + TmpDevolvidos.agencias + TmpDevolvidos.ncontas + TmpDevolvidos.ncheques
		
		If Seek(lcCheque,'dbDevolvido','Cheque')
			Replace dbDevolvido.numincdevs With dbDevolvido.numincdevs + 1
		Else
			Select TmpDevolvidos
			Scatter MemVar
			Insert Into dbDevolvido From MemVar
			Replace dbDevolvido.numincdevs With 1
		EndIf
	EndScan
	Use In TmpDevolvidos
	llTrue = .t.
	lcQuery = [Select a.Grupos, a.titulos, a.vencs, a.valors, a.valpags, a.moedas, a.Hist2s as Hists, ]+;
			  [(a.Valors - a.ValPags) as Saldos From SigMvCcr a, SigOpOpe b ]+;
			  [Where a.Dopcs = b.Dopes And b.Congvs = ?llTrue And a.Contems = ']+lcCdConta+[' And a.Opers = Left(b.Opers,1) ]+;
			  [And a.pagos = '1' and a.tipos = ' '] 
*!*				  Iif(Empty(ldDataIni) or Empty(ldDataFin),[],[and a.Datas between ?ldDataIni and ?ldDataFin])			  

	lnQueryOk = ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'TmpTit')
	If lnQueryOk < 1
		MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
		Return(.F.)
	EndIf

	Select DbTitulo
	Append From Dbf('TmpTit')

	Select CrSigCdGcr
	Go Top
	=Seek(CrSigCdPam.grpadclis,'CrSigCdGcr','Codigos')

	If CrSigCdGcr.limcres = 1
		Select CrSigCdCot
		Set Order To CMoeData Descending

		Select crSigClLcr
		Set Order To ('') In crSigClLcr
		Locate For Grupos+Contas==_Grupo + _Conta

		_Limite = 0
		_MoeLim = CrSigCdPam.moedaps
		_CotLim = 1
		If Seek(_MoeLim,'CrSigCdCot')
			_CotLim = CrSigCdCot.valos
		EndIf

		If Not Eof('CrSigClLcr')
			If CrSigClLcr.moedas <> _MoeLim
				_CotSal = 1
				If Seek(CrSigClLcr.moedas,'CrSigCdCot')
					_CotSal = CrSigCdCot.valos
				EndIf
				_Limite = Round(CrSigClLcr.limcres * _CotSal / _CotLim,2)
			Else
				_Limite = CrSigClLcr.limcres
			EndIf
		EndIf

		_Saldo = 0
		lcGCMs = _Grupo + _Conta
		If Used('TmpSccr')
			Use In TmpSccr
		EndIf
		lStrQuery = [Select grupos, moedas, saldos From SigMvSlc Where Grupos = ']+_Grupo+[' And Contas=']+_Conta+[']
		lnQueryOk = ThisForm.podataMgr.SqlExecute(lStrQuery, 'TmpSccr')
		If lnQueryOk < 1
			MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
			Return(.F.)
		EndIf
		Select TmpSccr
		Go Top
		Scan
			_CotSal = 1
			If Seek(TmpSccr.moedas,'CrSigCdCot')
				_CotSal = CrSigCdCot.valos
			EndIf
			If TmpSccr.moedas <> _MoeLim
				_Saldo = _Saldo + Round(TmpSccr.saldos * _CotSal / _CotLim,2)
			Else
				_Saldo = _Saldo + TmpSccr.saldos
			EndIf
		EndScan
		_Disp  = _Limite - _Saldo

		Select TmpTit
		Scan
			_CotSal = 1
			If Seek( Moedas, 'crSigCdCot' )
				_CotSal = crSigCdCot.Valos 
			Endif
			If Moedas # _MoeLim
				_ChqNeg = _ChqNeg + Round( Saldos * _CotSal / _CotLim, 2 )
			Else
				_ChqNeg = _ChqNeg + Saldos 
			Endif
		EndScan
		
		_Disp = _Disp - _ChqNeg

		If Used('TmpChe')
			Use In TmpChe
		EndIf
		lStrQuery = [Select grclis, iclis, valors, fpags, vencs, Umovs From SigChe ]+;
					[Where grclis = ']+_Grupo+[' And iclis = ']+_Conta+[' ]
		lnQueryOk = ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lStrQuery, 'TmpChe')
		If lnQueryOk < 1
			MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
			Return(.F.)
		EndIf

		Select a.grclis, a.iclis, a.valors, a.fpags, Nvl(a.vencs,Ctod(Space(8))) As vencs, b.devolvidos ;
		From TmpChe a, CrSigCdOpt b ;
		Where a.umovs = b.operacaos And b.tipos = 'DB' ;
		Into Cursor Cheques
		  
		_ChequeC = 0
		_ChequeP = 0
		_ChqAv   = 0
		_ChqVc   = 0
		_ChqDv   = 0
		Select Cheques
		Scan
			If empty(Cheques.fpags)
				_ChequeP = _ChequeP + Cheques.valors
			Else
				=Seek(Cheques.fpags,'CrSigOpFp','FPags')
				If CrSigOpFp.valpends <> 1
					_ChequeC = _ChequeC + Cheques.valors
				Else
					_ChequeP = _ChequeP + Cheques.valors
				EndIf
			EndIf
			_ChqAv = _ChqAv + Iif(Cheques.vencs >= Date(),Cheques.valors,0)
			_ChqVc = _ChqVc + Iif(Cheques.vencs < Date(),Cheques.valors,0)
			_ChqDv = _ChqDv + Iif(Cheques.devolvidos = 'S',Cheques.valors,0)
		EndScan
		_ChequeC = Round(_ChequeC / _CotLim,2)
		_ChequeP = Round(_ChequeP / _CotLim,2)
		_ChqAv   = Round(_ChqAv   / _CotLim,2)
		_ChqVc   = Round(_ChqVc   / _CotLim,2)
		_ChqDv   = Round(_ChqDv   / _CotLim,2)

		If CrSigCdPam.chqlCreds
			_Disp = _Disp - _ChequeC
		EndIf
		Use In Cheques
	EndIf

	If crSigCdGcr.LimEstoqs = 1 And !Empty( crSigCdPam.MoeLimes )

		_MoeLime = crSigCdPam.MoeLimes
		_CotLime = fBuscarCotacao( crSigCdPam.MoeLimes, Date(),ThisForm.Podatamgr )

		lcQuery = [Select a.Cpros, Sum(a.Sqtds) as Qtds From SigMvEst a, SigCdGcr b ]+;
				[Where a.Estos = ']+_Conta+[' And a.Grupos = b.Codigos And b.ChkLimEsts <> 2 ]+;
				[Group By a.cpros]

		If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'TmpEst') < 1)
			=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (TmpEst)')
			Return .f.
		EndIf

		Select TmpEst 

		Scan
		
			=ThisForm.PoDataMgr.CursorQuery('SigCdPro','CrTmpPro','Cpros',TmpEst.Cpros, [Cpros, Moevs, pvens, fvendas, moepvs, pesoms, custofs, moecusfs, Linhas])
			=ThisForm.PoDataMgr.CursorQuery('SigCdLin','CrTmpLin','Linhas',CrTmpPro.Linhas)

			If crSigCdPam.TpPrecos = 1
				m.moeEst = CrTmpPro.Moevs
				m.vlEst  = Round( CrTmpPro.pvens * Iif( CrTmpPro.fvendas # 0 And !IsEmpty( CrTmpPro.moepvs ), CrTmpPro.fvendas, 1 ) , 2 )
				If CrTmpLin.TpVendas = '2'
					m.vlEst = CrTmpPro.Pesoms * m.vlEst
				Endif
			Else
				m.vlEst = CrTmpPro.Custofs
				If CrTmpLin.TpCustos = '2'
					m.vlEst = CrTmpPro.Pesoms * m.vlEst
				Endif
				m.moeEst = CrTmpPro.Moecusfs
			Endif
			m.vlEst = m.vlEst * TmpEst.Qtds
			
			If _MoeLim # m.MoeEst
				_vlEstq = _vlEstq + ( m.vlEst * fBuscarCotacao( m.moeEst, Date(),ThisForm.PodataMgr ) / _CotLim )
			Else
				_vlEstq = _vlEstq + m.VlEst
			Endif
			
		EndScan

		_Disp = _Disp - _VlEstq

	Endif

	ThisForm.Fld_Principal.pag_resultado.Result.Cheque.Get_LCred.Value      = _Limite
	ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Cheque.Get_ChqAv.Value      = _ChqAv
	ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Cheque.Get_ChqVc.Value      = _Chqvc
	ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Cheque.Get_ChqDv.Value      = _ChqDv
	ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Cheque.Get_SaldoCc.Value    = _Saldo
	ThisForm.fld_principal.pag_resultado.Result.Cheque.get_ChqNeg.Value     = _ChqNeg
	ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Cheque.Get_Dispon.Value     = _Disp
	ThisForm.fld_principal.pag_resultado.Result.Cheque.Get_VLEstoque.Value = _VlEstq
	ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Cheque.MoedaConta.Caption   = _MoeLim
	ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Cheque.MoedaHis.Caption     = _MoeLim
	ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Cheque.Get_Dispon.Forecolor = Iif(_Disp < 0,RGB(255,0,0),RGB(0,0,255))

	Select dbDevolvido 
	Sum dbDevolvido.valors To _TotDev
	Go Top

	Go Top In dbDevolvido

	Select dbAberto
	Sum Valors To lnVal
	ThisForm.fld_principal.pag_resultado.result.Cheque.fwget3.Value = lnVal
	ThisForm.fld_principal.pag_resultado.result.Cheque.fwget3.Refresh
	Go Bottom
	
	ThisForm.OkCartao   = .F.
	ThisForm.OkProduto  = .F.
	ThisForm.OkEstoque  = .F. 

	*-[ Faz o processamento das outras opcoes ]-*
	With  ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result
		ThisForm.ConsultaExecutada = .T.
		if llSemDados
			.Cartao.Click()
			If Reccount('TmpCart') = 0
				.Vendas.Activate()
				.Conserto.Click()
				.Producao.Click()
			EndIf
			If Reccount('dbProduto') = 0 And Reccount('dbConserto') = 0
				.Estoque.Click()
			EndIf
		EndIf
		ThisForm.ConsultaExecutada = .F.
	EndWith

	cListFile = "|dbSaldo    |TmpPar     |dbAberto   |dbDevolvido|TmpCart    |dbProduto  |dbEstoque  |dbConserto |dbOpera    "
	cListPage = "| 1         | 2         | 3         |  3        | 4         |  5        | 6         | 7         | 8         "
