------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
*!*	Wait Window 'Aguarde! Verificando Históricos...' NoWait

*!*	Zap in DbHistorico 
*!*	Zap in DbSaldo
*!*	Zap In TmpPar	
*!*	Zap in dbSaldo
*!*		 
*!*	lcCdConta = Padr(ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Consulta.Origem.Get_Conta.Value,10)
*!*	ldDataIni = ''
*!*	ldDataFin = ''
*!*	If !Empty(thisform.fld_principal.pag_resultado.result.saLDO.get_dt_inicial.Value) and !Empty(thisform.fld_principal.pag_resultado.result.saLDO.get_dt_final.Value)
*!*		ldDataIni = fDtoSQL(thisform.fld_principal.pag_resultado.result.saLDO.get_dt_inicial.Value)
*!*		ldDataFin = fDtoSQL(thisform.fld_principal.pag_resultado.result.saLDO.get_dt_final.Value,'23:59:59')
*!*	EndIf

*!*	If Used('TmpMccr')
*!*		Use In TmpMccr
*!*	EndIf
*!*	lStrQuery = [Select * From SigMvCcr ]+;
*!*				[Where contas = ']+lcCdConta+[' ] +;
*!*				Iif(Empty(ldDataIni) or Empty(ldDataFin),[],[and Datas between ?ldDataIni and ?ldDataFin])
*!*	If ThisForm.podataMgr.SqlExecute(lStrQuery, 'TmpMccr') < 1
*!*		MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo !!!',16,'Falha na Conexão - (TmpMccr)')
*!*		Return(.F.)
*!*	EndIf

*!*	Select TmpMccr 
*!*	Scan
*!*		Insert Into DbHistorico (Emps, Grupos, Datas, Valors, Opers, Saldos, Hists, Hist2s, Moedas, Dopes, Numes) ;
*!*				Values (TmpMccr.Emps, TmpMccr.Grupos, Nvl(TmpMccr.Datas,Ctod(Space(08))), TmpMccr.Valors, ;
*!*						TmpMccr.Opers, TmpMccr.Saldos, TmpMccr.Hists, TmpMccr.Hist2s, TmpMccr.Moedas, ;
*!*						TmpMccr.Dopes, TmpMccr.Numes)
*!*		llSemDados = .f.
*!*		
*!*		If Nopers = 0 Or !Inlist( Tipos, ' ', 'X' ) Or (!Empty( Dopes ) And !Empty( Numes ) And Tipos # 'X')
*!*			Loop
*!*		Endif

*!*		_Valos = ( TmpMccr.Valors - TmpMccr.Valpags )
*!*		If _Valos > 0 
*!*			Insert Into TmpPar ( Emps, Grupos, Vencs, Fpags, Valos, Moefpgs, Hists, Opers ) ;
*!*					Values ( TmpMccr.Emps, TmpMccr.Grupos, Nvl(TmpMccr.Vencs,Ctod(Space(08))), 'C/Corrente',;
*!*							 _Valos, TmpMccr.Moedas, TmpMccr.Hists, TmpMccr.Opers )
*!*			=ThisForm.Carrega_Combo( TmpMccr.Grupos, ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Pendencias.Cmb_Grupo, 'TmpPar' )
*!*		EndIf 
*!*	EndScan

*!*	If ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Pendencias.Cmb_Grupo.ListCount = 0
*!*		Zap In TmpPar	
*!*	EndIf 

*!*	Select grupos, moedas, Sum(Valors * Iif(opers=[D],1,-1)) as saldos ;
*!*	from DbHistorico group by grupos, moedas into cursor TmpSccr

*!*	Select TmpSccr
*!*	Scan
*!*		Insert Into dbSaldo (grupos,moedas,saldos) Values (TmpSccr.grupos,TmpSccr.moedas,TmpSccr.saldos)
*!*		llSemDados = .F.
*!*		=ThisForm.Carrega_Combo(TmpSccr.grupos,;
*!*			ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Saldo.Cmb_Grupo,'dbSaldo')
*!*	EndScan 
*!*	Go Top In dbSaldo

*!*	If ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Saldo.Cmb_Grupo.ListCount = 0
*!*		Zap In dbSaldo	
*!*	EndIf 

*!*	thisform.fld_principal.pag_resultado.result.saLDO.Refresh()
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE LostFocus
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE LostFocus
	thisform.ConsultaExecutada = .f.
	ThisForm.Processa()

	ThisForm.LockScreen = .T.

	With ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado
		.cntConta.Visible    = .F.
		.cntConta.Enabled    = .F.
		.Result.Visible      = .T.
		.Result.Enabled      = .T.
		.Result.ActivePage   = ThisForm.ResultPageToSet
		.Imprime.Visible     = .T.
		.Imprime.Enabled     = .T.
		.Cmd_MudaMov.Visible = .F.
		.Cmd_MudaMov.Enabled = .F.
		
		.Cmd_MudaCli.Visible = ThisForm.fld_principal.pag_consulta.grp_operacao.Cmd_Cadastro.Visible
		.Cmd_MudaCli.Enabled = ThisForm.fld_principal.pag_consulta.grp_operacao.Cmd_Cadastro.Enabled
		.Result.Refresh

		If pcMensagem
			.Cmd_MudaCli.Visible = .F.
			.Cmd_MudaCli.Enabled = .F.
			.Cmd_MudaMov.Visible = .F.
			.Cmd_MudaMov.Enabled = .F.
			.Imprime.Visible     = .F.
			.Imprime.Enabled     = .F.
			.Result.Visible      = .F.
			.Result.Enabled      = .F.
			.cntConta.Visible    = .T.
			.cntConta.Enabled    = .T.
		EndIf
		.Refresh
		
	EndWith

*!*		ThisForm.Fld_Principal.ActivePage = 2
	ThisForm.Refresh
	ThisForm.LockScreen = .F.

ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPCGV.fld_principal.pag_resultado.grp_operacao
* Classe: commandgroup (Base: commandgroup)
* Registro: 518
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE cmd_voltar.Click
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE cmd_voltar.Click

**** Incluido - Mena - 13/10/2011 - permite alteração de dados de cliente ****
IF ThisForm.pcEscolha = 'ALTERAR'
	IF MessageBox('Deseja salvar as alterações?',32+4,'Alteração de Cliente') = 6
		IF thisform.fld_principal.pag_resultado.CntConta.mvalidaobj() < 1
			RETURN .f.
		endif  
		thisform.fld_principal.pag_resultado.CntConta.mgravadados()
	ENDIF
ENDIF
*******************************************************************************

ThisForm.pccredito=.f. 

If ThisForm.Automatico
	ThisForm.Release

	If Type('ThisForm.poForm1') == 'O' And Not ThisForm.poForm1.Enabled
		ThisForm.poForm1.Enabled = .t.

	EndIf 

Else
	ThisForm.Fld_Principal.ActivePage = 1

	With This.Parent.Parent.Parent.pag_consulta.Origem
		.Get_conta.Value = Space(10)
		.Get_dconta.Value = Space(50)
		.Get_cpf.Value = Space(20)
		.GetConj.Value = Space(40)
		.getTelefone.Value = Space(15)
		thisform.Height = 300		
		.Refresh
		.Get_Conta.SetFocus

	EndWith 

EndIf

ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPCGV.fld_principal.pag_resultado.Cmd_MudaCli
* Classe: commandgroup (Base: commandgroup)
* Registro: 519
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE cmd_cadastro.Click
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE cmd_cadastro.Click
pcMensagem = .T.
ThisForm.LockScreen = .T.
With ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado
	.cntConta.Visible = .T.
	.cntConta.Enabled = .T.
	.Result.Visible   = .F.
	.Result.Enabled   = .F.
	.Imprime.Visible  = fChecaAcesso('SIGOPCGV','IMPRIMIR')
	.Imprime.Enabled  = .Imprime.Visible
	If fChecaAcesso('SIGOPCGV','MOVIMENTA')
		.Cmd_MudaMov.Enabled = .T.
		.Cmd_MudaMov.Visible = .T.
	EndIf
	.Cmd_MudaCli.Enabled = .F.
	.Cmd_MudaCli.Visible = .F.
	.cnt_periodo.Enabled = .F.
	.cnt_periodo.Visible = .F.
	.optiongroup1.Enabled = .F.
	.optiongroup1.Visible = .F.
EndWith
ThisForm.LockScreen = .F.
ThisForm.pcEscolha = IIF(fChecaAcesso('SIGOPCGV', 'ALTERAR'), 'ALTERAR','CONSULTAR')

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE cmd_movimenta.Click
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE cmd_movimenta.Click
pcMensagem = .F.
ThisForm.LockScreen = .T.

With ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado
	.cntConta.Visible = .F.
	.cntConta.Enabled = .F.
	.Result.Visible   = .T.
	.Result.Enabled   = .T.
	.Imprime.Visible  = fChecaAcesso('SIGOPCGV','IMPRIMIR')
	.Imprime.Enabled  = .Imprime.Visible
	If fChecaAcesso('SIGOPCGV','CADASTRO')
		.Cmd_MudaCli.Enabled = .T.
		.Cmd_MudaCli.Visible = .T.
	EndIf
	.Cmd_MudaMov.Enabled = .F.
	.Cmd_MudaMov.Visible = .F.
	.cnt_periodo.Enabled = .T.
	.cnt_periodo.Visible = .T.
	.optiongroup1.Enabled = .T.
	.optiongroup1.Visible = .T.	
EndWith
ThisForm.LockScreen = .F.

ThisForm.pcEscolha = 'CONSULTAR'

ENDPROC
